for the sharper exact-family floor $g_a\ge 1.3537$. Thus the remaining microscopic theorem blocker is no longer a joint gap-plus-defect problem on the audited knot set: the on-shell defect is already effectively zero there, and the unresolved parent-side task is the gap / lift statement itself.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O3 (Audited bath-family rigidity source for the exact lift).}
The surviving gap / lift statement also admits a sharper audit-level source than the earlier conditional phrasing might suggest. Across the four explicit parent-bath deformation scans already exported on the same knot set---the linear family audit, the generator-affinity audit, the log-coordinate audit, and the normal-coordinate audit---the exact statement is always selected at the undeformed point. Concretely, the audited minimizers are
\[
(m,u,v)=(0,0,0),
\qquad
(q_{ss},q_{bb},q_{sb})=(0,0,0),
\]
\[
(p_{\rm sys},p_{\rm spec})=(0,0),
\qquad
(\zeta_{\rm sys},\zeta_{\rm spec})=(0,0).
\]
The corresponding canonical objectives stay at
\[
J_{\rm can}\le 3.18\times10^{-12},
\]
while the smallest audited runner-up gap among those four scans is already
\[
\Delta J_{\rm sel}\ge 6.90\times10^{-4}.
\]
Hence the exact parent-bath statement is canonically rigid on the present audit scope: the weakest observed deformation gap still exceeds the continuum-neighborhood tolerance $J_\ast=10^{-6}$ from Proposition~O5 by a factor
\[
\Delta J_{\rm sel}/J_\ast \ge 6.90\times10^{2},
\]
and exceeds the largest canonical residual by
\[
\Delta J_{\rm sel}/J_{\rm can}^{\max}\ge 2.17\times10^{8}.
\]
This rigidity is consistent with the companion exactness audits,
\[
\max\bigl|\log(\gamma_{\rm mix}/\gamma_\phi)-(\log\mathcal A_{\rm sys}+\log\mathcal A_{\rm bath})\bigr|
\le 8.43\times10^{-13},
\]
\[
\max |\Delta_{\rm cocycle}^{\rm mix}|\le 1.11\times10^{-15},
\qquad
\max |\Delta_{\rm chain}^{\rm mix}|\le 4.03\times10^{-15},
\]
from the parent-bath statement, cocycle, and potential/chain reconstructions. The new consolidation audit \path{code/audit_open_system_exact_lift_source.py} therefore supports the following reviewer-facing reading: on the current audited knot set, the continuum neighborhood budget of Proposition~O5 is not merely an arbitrary local ansatz, but is already compatible with the uniquely selected exact parent-bath statement. Using the same audited budget
\[
u_a\le 5.34\times10^{-3}
\]
therefore leaves the exact lifted floor
\[
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339,
\qquad
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9947\,H_{PP,a},
\]
and, together with Corollary~O2, forces only
\[
1-\nu_{1,a}\le 5.12\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\varepsilon_a(\Delta E_a)\le 2.97\times10^{-14}
\]
on the audited knot set. In this narrowed audit-level sense, the exact-lift source is no longer a free-standing mystery: the remaining proof-level step is to replace this canonical rigidity evidence by a genuinely operator-level theorem, not to discover a new gap scale.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O6 (Canonical-rigidity transfer on the audited family classes).}
The previous corollary can be sharpened into a conditional transfer statement on the explicit deformation families already scanned. Let $\mathfrak F$ denote any one of the four audited parent-bath classes
\[
\mathfrak F\in\{\text{linear family},\ \text{generator-affinity},\ \text{log-coordinate},\ \text{normal-coordinate}\}.
\]
Suppose an exact Schur-side candidate remains inside one such family and satisfies the same local tolerance
\[
J_{\rm parent}\le J_\ast=10^{-6}.
\]
Then on the current audit grid the candidate is forced back to the undeformed canonical point of that family. Equivalently, within each of the four audited classes the canonical point is the unique admissible point at the theorem tolerance.

\textit{Audit proof.} The new transfer audit \path{code/audit_open_system_rigidity_transfer.py} checks the full exported scan tables directly rather than only comparing runner-up gaps. At $J_\ast=10^{-6}$ it finds, for all four families,
\[
N_{\rm adm}^{\rm(total)}=1,
\qquad
N_{\rm adm}^{\rm(noncan)}=0,
\]
so the only surviving point is the undeformed canonical one. Quantitatively, the largest canonical objective among the four scans is still only
\[
J_{\rm can}^{\max}=3.18\times10^{-12},
\]
while the smallest non-canonical objective is already
\[
J_{\rm noncan}^{\min}=6.90\times10^{-4},
\]
hence
\[
J_{\rm noncan}^{\min}-J_{\rm can}^{\max}\ge 6.90\times10^{-4}.
\]
This leaves the same margins as before,
\[
\frac{J_{\rm noncan}^{\min}-J_{\rm can}^{\max}}{J_\ast}\ge 6.90\times10^{2},
\qquad
\frac{J_{\rm noncan}^{\min}-J_{\rm can}^{\max}}{J_{\rm can}^{\max}}\ge 2.17\times10^{8},
\]
but now in the stronger form that no non-canonical family member survives the theorem tolerance at all. Therefore, conditional on the exact operator being represented by any of these audited deformation classes, the continuum-neighborhood determinant-deficit budget of Proposition~O5 transfers unchanged to the exact Schur-reduced operator on the audited knot set.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O4 (Exact-lift consequences on the audited family classes).}
Under the family-class hypothesis of Proposition~O6, the exact Schur-reduced operator inherits the same budget
\[
u_a\le 5.34\times10^{-3},
\]
and therefore the same operator consequences,
\[
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339,
\qquad
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9947\,H_{PP,a},
\]
on the audited knot set. Together with Corollary~O2 this leaves only
\[
1-\nu_{1,a}\le 5.12\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\varepsilon_a(\Delta E_a)\le 2.97\times10^{-14}.
\]
Thus, on the currently exported family classes, the unresolved theorem gap is no longer whether the lift survives, but whether one can remove the explicit family-class hypothesis and prove the same transfer directly at the operator level.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O7 (Operator-witness chart transfer on the audited chart).}
The preceding family-class hypothesis can be weakened one step further by replacing the explicit deformation labels with operator witnesses. Define the p95 and max witness vectors
\[
W_{a,95}:=
(\Delta^{95}_{11,a},\,\Delta^{95}_{22,a},\,\Delta^{95}_{{\rm tr},a},\,\Delta^{95}_{{\rm det},a},\,\Delta^{95}_{{\rm ani},a}),
\]
\[
W_{a,\max}:=
(\Delta^{\max}_{11,a},\,\Delta^{\max}_{22,a},\,\Delta^{\max}_{{\rm tr},a},\,\Delta^{\max}_{{\rm det},a},\,\Delta^{\max}_{{\rm ani},a}),
\]
where the entries are exactly the block, trace, determinant, and anisotropy residuals already used in the parent-bath family scans. Suppose an exact Schur-side candidate satisfies the same local tolerance
\[
J_{\rm parent}\le J_\ast=10^{-6},
\]
and lies in the audited operator witness chart
\[
\|W_{a,95}\|_\infty \le 9.62\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\|W_{a,\max}\|_\infty \le 1.01\times10^{-4}.
\]
Then, on the current audited chart, the candidate belongs to the same canonical pocket as the undeformed point and therefore inherits the Proposition~O5 determinant-deficit budget.

\textit{Audit proof.} The new aggregation audit \path{code/audit_open_system_operator_witness_transfer.py} discards the family labels and stacks all non-canonical rows from the four exported scans directly in witness space. Across that union, the nearest non-canonical challenger is already the generator-affinity point \(q_{bb}=\pm 0.0625\), with
\[
\min_{\rm noncan}\|W_{a,95}\|_\infty = 1.92\times10^{-4},
\qquad
\min_{\rm noncan}\|W_{a,\max}\|_\infty = 2.01\times10^{-4}.
\]
The canonical witness suprema remain only
\[
\|W_{a,95}^{\rm(can)}\|_\infty \le 8.17\times10^{-13},
\qquad
\|W_{a,\max}^{\rm(can)}\|_\infty \le 8.43\times10^{-13},
\]
so the half-radius chart bounds above still leave margins of
\[
\frac{9.62\times10^{-5}}{\|W_{a,95}^{\rm(can)}\|_\infty}\ge 1.18\times10^{8},
\qquad
\frac{1.01\times10^{-4}}{\|W_{a,\max}^{\rm(can)}\|_\infty}\ge 1.19\times10^{8}.
\]
Therefore, on the audited operator chart, any exact candidate satisfying the same theorem tolerance and these witness-radius bounds cannot land on any scanned non-canonical deformation and is forced into the canonical pocket. This removes the explicit family-class labels from the transfer statement, even though the last remaining proof step is still to justify the witness-radius bound directly from the exact operator.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O5 (Exact-lift consequences on the audited operator chart).}
Under the witness-chart hypothesis of Proposition~O7, the exact Schur-reduced operator again inherits the same neighborhood budget
\[
u_a\le 5.34\times10^{-3},
\]
and hence the same operator consequences,
\[
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339,
\qquad
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9947\,H_{PP,a},
\]
on the audited knot set. Together with Corollary~O2 this again leaves only
\[
1-\nu_{1,a}\le 5.12\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\varepsilon_a(\Delta E_a)\le 2.97\times10^{-14}.
\]
Thus the remaining theorem gap is now narrower still: derive the witness-radius bound directly from the exact operator, rather than from the exported scan chart.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O6 (The exported exact operator lies inside the audited witness chart).}
The chart hypothesis of Proposition~O7 is itself now verified directly on the exported exact operator data. Reading the exact parent-bath statement map rowwise and aggregating it with the same p95/max conventions used in the family scans gives
\[
\|W_{95}^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty = 3.52\times10^{-7},
\qquad
\|W_{\max}^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty = 3.63\times10^{-7},
\]
with the dominant component in both cases coming from the exact block-22 witness. These are already smaller than the audited chart radii of Proposition~O7 by factors
\[
\frac{9.62\times10^{-5}}{\|W_{95}^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty}=2.73\times10^{2},
\qquad
\frac{1.01\times10^{-4}}{\|W_{\max}^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty}=2.77\times10^{2}.
\]
The companion exactness witnesses remain tiny,
\[
\Delta_{\rm cocycle}^{\max}=1.11\times10^{-15},
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm chain}^{\max}=4.03\times10^{-15},
\]
so the exported exact operator already lies well inside the audited witness chart. Consequently the conclusions of Corollary~O5 apply directly to the exported exact Schur-reduced operator on the audited knot set:
\[
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339,
\qquad
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9947\,H_{PP,a},
\]
and, together with Corollary~O2,
\[
1-\nu_{1,a}\le 5.12\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\varepsilon_a(\Delta E_a)\le 2.97\times10^{-14}.
\]
The remaining proof-level gap is now very specific: promote this verified chart inclusion of the exported exact operator into an analytic operator theorem.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O8 (Exact-equation witness envelope for the exported operator).}
The preceding chart inclusion can be compressed one step further into a single operator-envelope bound that no longer needs the rowwise chart aggregation. Define
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}:=
\max\bigl\{
\Delta_{\phi}^{\rm(fact)},
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)},
\Delta_{\rm tr}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm det}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm ani}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(cocycle)},
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(potential)}
\bigr\},
\]
with
\[
\Delta_{\phi}^{\rm(fact)}:=\max|\kappa_{\phi}^{\rm(obs)}-\kappa_{\rm env}|,
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}:=\max|\kappa_{\rm mix}^{\rm(obs)}-\kappa_{\rm env}|,
\]
\[
\Delta_{\rm tr}^{\rm(stmt)}:=\max|{\rm tr}(K_{\rm bath})-{\rm tr}(\Gamma)|,
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm det}^{\rm(stmt)}:=\max|\det(K_{\rm bath})-\det(\Gamma)|,
\]
\[
\Delta_{\rm ani}^{\rm(stmt)}:=\max\bigl|\log(\gamma_{\rm mix}/\gamma_{\phi})-\log({\rm sys})-\log({\rm bath})\bigr|,
\]
and where the last two terms are the exact mix-side cocycle and potential envelopes already exported by the parent-bath closure audits. Then the exact witness equations imply
\[
\|W^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty \le \mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}.
\]

\textit{Audit proof.} \path{code/audit_open_system_exact_operator_radius_bound.py} reads the factorization, statement, cocycle, and potential summaries directly and evaluates the envelope components
\[
\Delta_{\phi}^{\rm(fact)}=2.33\times10^{-10},
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}=3.63\times10^{-7},
\]
\[
\Delta_{\rm tr}^{\rm(stmt)}=4.40\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm det}^{\rm(stmt)}=4.21\times10^{-18},
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm ani}^{\rm(stmt)}=8.43\times10^{-13},
\]
\[
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(cocycle)}=8.64\times10^{-13},
\qquad
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(potential)}=5.50\times10^{-13}.
\]
Hence
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}=3.63\times10^{-7},
\]
with the envelope dominated by the exact factorization-side block-22 witness. The same audit checks that this conservative envelope is tight on the exported operator, i.e.
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}=\|W_{\max}^{\rm(exact)}\|_\infty,
\]
so no hidden larger witness remains outside the listed exact equations.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O7 (Half-radius chart inclusion from the exact witness equations).}
The envelope of Proposition~O8 is already smaller than one half of both audited chart radii:
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}
=3.63\times10^{-7}
\le \frac{1}{2}r_{95}^{\rm safe}=4.81\times10^{-5},
\]
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}
=3.63\times10^{-7}
\le \frac{1}{2}r_{\max}^{\rm safe}=5.03\times10^{-5}.
\]
Equivalently,
\[
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}}{\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}}=1.33\times10^{2},
\qquad
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}}{\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}}=1.39\times10^{2}.
\]
Therefore the exported exact operator satisfies the witness-chart hypothesis of Proposition~O7 with half-radius room to spare, directly from the exact factorization, statement, cocycle, and potential equations themselves rather than from the scan chart alone. Consequently the exact-lift conclusions of Corollary~O5 follow again for the exported operator on the audited knot set. The proof-level gap is now reduced to a final analytic upgrade: replace these exported exact residual constants by an operator theorem that bounds the same envelope a priori.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O9 (Mix-side factorization witness half-radius bound).}
The dominant term in the envelope of Proposition~O8 can itself be reduced to a simple quotient bound. Rowwise,
\[
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}(D)
:=
\left|\frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)}-\kappa_{\rm env}\right|
=
\frac{\bigl|\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)-\kappa_{\rm env}\,g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)\bigr|}
{g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)}.
\]
Therefore any uniform numerator bound
\[
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}
\ge
\max_D \bigl|\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)-\kappa_{\rm env}\,g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)\bigr|
\]
and any positive lower floor
\[
m_{\rm mix}\le \min_D g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)
\]
imply the operator bound
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le \frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{m_{\rm mix}}.
\]

\textit{Audit proof.} \path{code/audit_open_system_mix_factorization_source.py} evaluates this quotient source directly on the exported exact factorization map. The common numerator bound is
\[
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}=4.40\times10^{-14}.
\]
Three natural lower floors for the denominator all remain positive on the audited knot set:
\[
m_{\rm mix}^{\rm(dir)}=\min_D g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)=1.2147\times10^{-9},
\]
\[
m_{\rm mix}^{(\gamma)}=\frac{\min_D\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)-\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}=1.2147\times10^{-9},
\]
\[
m_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep)}=\bigl(\min_D g_x(D)\bigr)^2\min_D S_{xx}(D)=1.1417\times10^{-9}.
\]
Hence
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{m_{\rm mix}^{\rm(dir)}}
=3.62\times10^{-5},
\]
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{m_{\rm mix}^{(\gamma)}}
=3.62\times10^{-5},
\]
and even the fully separated floor still gives
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{m_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep)}}
=3.85\times10^{-5}.
\]
All three bounds are already below the Proposition~O7 half-radii
\[
\frac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}=4.81\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\frac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}=5.03\times10^{-5}.
\]
So the dominant witness component itself now satisfies the audited half-radius condition from a simple factorization numerator/denominator estimate.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O8 (Remaining analytic gap reduced to a lower floor on the mix kernel).}
Proposition~O9 shows that the dominant block-22 witness no longer requires the full chart machinery: on the exported exact operator it is already controlled by one small numerator residual and one positive lower floor on $g_x^2S_{xx}$ (equivalently on $\gamma_{\rm mix}$ after dividing by $\kappa_{\rm env}$). Since all other witness components are already much smaller by Proposition~O8, the remaining proof-level gap is now narrower again:
\[
\text{prove an a priori lower bound on }g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)
\quad\text{or equivalently on }\gamma_{\rm mix}(D),
\]
strong enough to reproduce one of the denominator floors above. Once that is available, the half-radius chart inclusion of the exact operator follows without any remaining appeal to exported scan geometry.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O10 (Observable-chain source for the \texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm mix}$}{gamma_mix} floor).}
The mix rate also admits an exact observable-chain reconstruction:
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)
=
\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)\,\chi_{\rm eff}(D)}{2\,C_{\max}(D)}.
\]
Hence any lower floors
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)\ge \gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor},
\qquad
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge \chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor},
\qquad
C_{\max}(D)\le C_{\max}^{\rm ceil},
\]
imply the mix-rate floor
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)\ge
\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}\,\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}
{2\,C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}}.
\]
Dividing by $\kappa_{\rm env}$ then yields a lower bound on the mix kernel,
\[
g_x(D)^2 S_{xx}(D)\ge
\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}\,\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}
{2\,C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}\,\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}.
\]

\textit{Audit proof.} \path{code/audit_open_system_gamma_mix_floor_source.py} verifies the exact identity above with
\[
\max_D\left|
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)-\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)\chi_{\rm eff}(D)}{2C_{\max}(D)}
\right|
=
4.12\times10^{-14}.
\]
On the exported exact operator, the conservative separated floors are
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}=4.75\times10^{2},
\qquad
\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}=9.03\times10^{-7},
\qquad
C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}=4.98\times10^{-1},
\]
which give
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}
=
\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}{2C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}}
=4.31\times10^{-4}.
\]
The actual exact minimum is
\[
\min_D\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)=5.23\times10^{-4},
\]
so the separated observable-chain floor already captures the true minimum within a factor
\[
\frac{\min_D\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}}=1.21.
\]
Since $\kappa_{\rm env}=4.31\times10^{5}$ on the audited knot set, this induces the conservative kernel floor
\[
g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge 1.00\times10^{-9}.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O9 (Observable-chain floor implies the half-radius mix witness bound).}
Combining Proposition~O10 with the numerator residual
\[
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}=4.40\times10^{-14}
\]
of Proposition~O9 gives
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{1.00\times10^{-9}}
=4.39\times10^{-5}.
\]
This is still below both audited half-radii,
\[
4.39\times10^{-5}<\frac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}=4.81\times10^{-5},
\qquad
4.39\times10^{-5}<\frac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}=5.03\times10^{-5},
\]
with margins
\[
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}}{4.39\times10^{-5}}=1.09,
\qquad
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}}{4.39\times10^{-5}}=1.15.
\]
Thus even the separated observable-chain floor on $\gamma_{\rm mix}$ is already sufficient to recover the half-radius bound for the dominant block-22 witness. The remaining proof-level task is therefore yet narrower: justify the individual floors on $\gamma_{\rm ref}$, $\chi_{\rm eff}$, and $C_{\max}$ (or a directly equivalent mix-rate floor) from the exact operator side.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O11 (First-mode source for the \texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm ref}$}{gamma_ref} floor).}
The reference rate is not an independent observable-chain input. In the localized-channel extraction it is defined by
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)=\sqrt{\Gamma_1(D)\Gamma_2(D)},
\qquad
\Gamma_1(D)=\omega_1(D)^9,
\qquad
\Gamma_2(D)=\omega_1(D)^{13},
\]
hence
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)=\omega_1(D)^{11}.
\]
On the audited open-system micro bridge one also has the gap-locked environmental timescale
\[
\tau_{\rm env}(D)=\omega_1(D)^{-1},
\]
so equivalently
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)=\tau_{\rm env}(D)^{-11}.
\]
Therefore any first-mode lower floor
\[
\omega_1(D)\ge \omega_1^{\rm floor}
\]
or, equivalently, any gap-locked timescale ceiling
\[
\tau_{\rm env}(D)\le \tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil}
\]
immediately implies
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)\ge (\omega_1^{\rm floor})^{11}=(\tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil})^{-11}.
\]

\textit{Audit proof.} \path{code/audit_open_system_gamma_ref_floor_source.py} verifies on the exported exact bridge that
\[
\max_D|\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)-\omega_1(D)^{11}|=1.56\times10^{-11},
\]
\[
\max_D|\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)-\tau_{\rm env}(D)^{-11}|=1.59\times10^{-11},
\]
\[
\max_D|\tau_{\rm env}(D)-\omega_1(D)^{-1}|=1.11\times10^{-16}.
\]
The exact audited floors/ceilings are
\[
\omega_1^{\rm floor}=1.751201\ldots,
\qquad
\tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil}=0.5710366\ldots,
\]
both attained at $D=4$, and they induce the same reference-rate floor
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}
=
(\omega_1^{\rm floor})^{11}
=
(\tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil})^{-11}
=
475.002983\ldots,
\]
which matches the actual exact minimum of $\gamma_{\rm ref}$ to machine precision.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O10 (\texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm ref}$}{gamma_ref} is no longer an independent blocker).}
Combining Proposition~O11 with Proposition~O10 shows that the $\gamma_{\rm ref}$ side of the observable-chain floor is already reduced to the first-mode floor $\omega_1^{\rm floor}$ (equivalently the gap-locked timescale ceiling $\tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil}$). In particular, on the current exact export the separated observable-chain estimate of Proposition~O10 can be rewritten with no additional loss as
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)\ge
\frac{(\omega_1^{\rm floor})^{11}\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}{2C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}}
=
\frac{(\tau_{\rm env}^{\rm ceil})^{-11}\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}{2C_{\max}^{\rm ceil}}.
\]
So after Proposition~O11, the remaining proof-level task narrows again: $\gamma_{\rm ref}$ no longer needs a separate source argument, and the residual observable-chain closure is concentrated on the floor/ceiling pair $(\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor},C_{\max}^{\rm ceil})$ or on a directly equivalent mix-rate floor.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O12 (Universal two-level source for the \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} ceiling).}
Let $\rho(t)$ be any positive semidefinite $2\times 2$ density matrix with unit trace. Writing
\[
\rho(t)=
\begin{pmatrix}
\rho_{LL}(t) & \rho_{LR}(t) \\
\rho_{RL}(t) & \rho_{RR}(t)
\end{pmatrix},
\qquad
\rho_{LL}(t)+\rho_{RR}(t)=1,
\]
positivity implies
\[
|\rho_{LR}(t)|^2 \le \rho_{LL}(t)\rho_{RR}(t)
\le
\left(\frac{\rho_{LL}(t)+\rho_{RR}(t)}{2}\right)^2
=\frac14.
\]
Hence
\[
|\rho_{LR}(t)|\le \frac12
\qquad\text{for all }t,
\]
and therefore the open-system coherence response
\[
C_{\max}:=\max_t |\rho_{LR}(t)|
\]
obeys the universal ceiling
\[
C_{\max}\le \frac12.
\]

\textit{Audit check.} \path{code/audit_open_system_cmax_ceiling_source.py} verifies on the exported exact micro bridge that
\[
\max_D C_{\max}(D)=0.497539\ldots<\frac12,
\]
with strict slack
\[
\frac12-\max_D C_{\max}(D)=2.461\times10^{-3}.
\]
Equivalently,
\[
\frac14-\max_D C_{\max}(D)^2=2.455\times10^{-3}>0.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O11 (Universal \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} ceiling still implies the half-radius mix witness bound).}
Combining Proposition~O12 with Proposition~O11 and the observable-chain identity of Proposition~O10 yields
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)\ge
\frac{\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}}{2(\tfrac12)}
=
\gamma_{\rm ref}^{\rm floor}\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}.
\]
On the current exact export this gives the conservative floor
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)\ge 4.29102\times10^{-4},
\]
and therefore
\[
g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge 9.96645\times10^{-10}.
\]
Using again the common numerator residual
\[
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}=4.40\times10^{-14}
\]
from Proposition~O9, one obtains
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{9.96645\times10^{-10}}
=4.41\times10^{-5}.
\]
This remains below both half-radii,
\[
4.41\times10^{-5}<\frac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}=4.81\times10^{-5},
\qquad
4.41\times10^{-5}<\frac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}=5.03\times10^{-5},
\]
with margins
\[
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}}{4.41\times10^{-5}}=1.09,
\qquad
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{\max}^{\rm safe}}{4.41\times10^{-5}}=1.14.
\]
So after Proposition~O12, the universal two-level positivity bound already removes $C_{\max}$ as an independent proof-level obstacle. The residual observable-chain closure is now narrower one final time: it is concentrated on $\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm floor}$, or on a directly equivalent floor for $\gamma_{\rm mix}$.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O13 (Reduction of the \texorpdfstring{$\chi_{\rm eff}$}{chi_eff} floor to a coherence-response floor).}
On the exact micro bridge one has the exact identities
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)
=
\chi_{LR}(D)\,\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}(D)}{\chi_{LR}(D)}
=
\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)C_{\max}(D)}{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)},
\]
and, since $\chi_{LR}(D)=|M_{LR}(D)|/\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)$,
\[
\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}(D)}{\chi_{LR}(D)}
=
\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)C_{\max}(D)}{|M_{LR}(D)|}.
\]
Equivalently,
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)=A_\chi(D)\,C_{\max}(D),
\qquad
A_\chi(D):=\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)}
=
\kappa_{\rm env}\frac{2g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)}{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)}.
\]
Using Proposition~O11 to rewrite
\[
\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)=\omega_1(D)^{11},
\]
any first-mode ceiling $\omega_1(D)\le \omega_1^{\rm ceil}$ implies the separated prefactor floor
\[
A_\chi(D)\ge
\kappa_{\rm env}\,
\frac{2\,g_{x,\min}^2\,S_{xx}^{\rm floor}}{(\omega_1^{\rm ceil})^{11}}
:
A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}.
\]

\textit{Audit check.} \path{code/audit_open_system_chi_eff_floor_source.py} verifies on the exact exported bridge that
\[
\max_D\left|A_\chi-\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}}{\gamma_{\rm ref}}\right|=1.74\times10^{-16},
\qquad
\max_D\left|A_\chi-\kappa_{\rm env}\frac{2g_x^2S_{xx}}{\gamma_{\rm ref}}\right|=1.01\times10^{-16},
\]
\[
\max_D\left|\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}}{\chi_{LR}}-\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}C_{\max}}{|M_{LR}|}\right|
=4.72\times10^{-16},
\qquad
\max_D\left|\chi_{\rm eff}-\chi_{LR}\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}}{\chi_{LR}}\right|
=5.58\times10^{-17}.
\]
The same audit finds
\[
A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}=1.815669\times10^{-6},
\qquad
A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}=1.041474\times10^{-6},
\]
so the separated prefactor floor already matches the exact one within a factor
\[
\frac{A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}}{A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}}=1.743.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O12 (The remaining \texorpdfstring{$\chi_{\rm eff}$}{chi_eff} closure is exactly a \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} floor).}
Proposition~O13 shows that the residual open-system observable-chain problem can be rewritten as
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}\,C_{\max}(D).
\]
So once a coherence-response floor
\[
C_{\max}(D)\ge C_{\max}^{\rm floor}
\]
is available, one obtains immediately
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}\,C_{\max}^{\rm floor}.
\]
On the current exact export, using only the exact minimum
\[
C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}=0.2342548\ldots
\]
already gives the conservative bound
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge 2.43970\times10^{-7},
\]
while the actual exported floor is
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}=9.03367\times10^{-7},
\]
so even this coarse separated reduction undershoots the exact floor by only
\[
\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
{A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
=3.70.
\]
Hence after Propositions~O11--O13 the residual observable-chain closure is now fully localized: the only scalar still missing from the exact export to a complete proof-side floor statement is a lower bound on $C_{\max}$ itself.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O14 (Underdamped closed form for \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} on the exact two-level bridge).}
In the localized two-level Lindblad block used by the exact micro bridge,
\[
H=\frac{\Delta E}{2}\sigma_x,
\qquad
L_\phi=\sqrt{\gamma_\phi}\,\sigma_z,
\qquad
L_{\rm mix}=\sqrt{\gamma_{\rm mix}}\,\sigma_x,
\]
with initial state $\rho(0)=|L\rangle\langle L|$, the Bloch evolution closes on the $(y,z)$ plane:
\[
\dot y = -\Delta E\, z - 2(\gamma_\phi+\gamma_{\rm mix})y,
\qquad
\dot z = \Delta E\, y - 2\gamma_{\rm mix} z.
\]
Writing
\[
\Gamma := \gamma_\phi + 2\gamma_{\rm mix},
\qquad
\omega := \sqrt{\Delta E^2-\gamma_\phi^2},
\]
the underdamped regime $\Delta E>\gamma_\phi$ gives the exact solution
\[
y(t)=-\frac{\Delta E}{\omega}e^{-\Gamma t}\sin(\omega t),
\qquad
C(t)=|\rho_{LR}(t)|=\frac{|y(t)|}{2}
=
\frac{\Delta E}{2\omega}e^{-\Gamma t}|\sin(\omega t)|.
\]
The first-lobe maximum occurs at
\[
t_\ast = \omega^{-1}\arctan\!\left(\frac{\omega}{\Gamma}\right),
\]
so
\[
C_{\max}
=
\frac{\Delta E}{2\sqrt{\Gamma^2+\omega^2}}
\exp\!\left[
-\frac{\Gamma}{\omega}\arctan\!\left(\frac{\omega}{\Gamma}\right)
\right].
\]

\textit{Audit check.} \path{code/audit_open_system_cmax_floor_source.py} verifies on the exported exact bridge that all rows are underdamped,
\[
\min_D(\Delta E-\gamma_\phi)=2.59273\times10^{-1}>0,
\qquad
\max_D \frac{\gamma_\phi}{\Delta E}=2.07\times10^{-4},
\]
and that the closed form above matches the exported Lindblad maxima to within
\[
\max_D |C_{\max}^{\rm(export)}-C_{\max}^{\rm(closed)}|
=2.82\times10^{-4}.
\]
The closed-form minimum is attained at
\[
D=5,
\qquad
C_{\max}^{\rm(closed)\ floor}=2.34338\times10^{-1},
\]
while the exported exact minimum is
\[
C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}=2.34255\times10^{-1},
\]
so
\[
\frac{C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}}{C_{\max}^{\rm(closed)\ floor}}=0.999644.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O13 (Separated \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} floor and resulting \texorpdfstring{$\chi_{\rm eff}$}{chi_eff} floor).}
On the audited exact parameter box, the same closed form is numerically monotone:
\[
\partial_{\Delta E} C_{\max}>0,
\qquad
\partial_{\gamma_\phi} C_{\max}<0,
\qquad
\partial_{\gamma_{\rm mix}} C_{\max}<0,
\]
with audited derivative margins
\[
\begin{aligned}
\min \partial_{\Delta E} C_{\max}&=3.16\times10^{-3},\\
\max \partial_{\gamma_\phi} C_{\max}&=-3.98\times10^{-1},\\
\max \partial_{\gamma_{\rm mix}} C_{\max}&=-7.96\times10^{-1}.
\end{aligned}
\]
Therefore the exact bridge already admits the separated floor
\[
\begin{aligned}
C_{\max}(D)&\ge C_{\max}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}\\
&=
\frac{\Delta E_{\min}}
{2\sqrt{\Gamma_{\max}^2+\omega_{\min}^2}}
\exp\!\left[
-\frac{\Gamma_{\max}}{\omega_{\min}}
\arctan\!\left(\frac{\omega_{\min}}{\Gamma_{\max}}\right)
\right],
\end{aligned}
\]
where
\[
\Delta E_{\min}=2.59278\times10^{-1},
\qquad
\gamma_{\phi,\max}=9.56\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\gamma_{{\rm mix},\max}=1.48999\times10^{-1},
\]
\[
\omega_{\min}=\sqrt{\Delta E_{\min}^2-\gamma_{\phi,\max}^2}=2.59278\times10^{-1},
\qquad
\Gamma_{\max}=\gamma_{\phi,\max}+2\gamma_{{\rm mix},\max}=2.98093\times10^{-1},
\]
giving
\[
C_{\max}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}=1.44083\times10^{-1}.
\]
The exact exported floor exceeds this by
\[
\frac{C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}}{C_{\max}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}}=1.626.
\]
Combining this with Proposition~O13 gives the conservative observable floor
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge
A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)} C_{\max}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}
=1.50059\times10^{-7},
\]
while the actual exported exact floor is
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}=9.03367\times10^{-7},
\]
so
\[
\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
{A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)} C_{\max}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}}
=6.02.
\]
Thus after Propositions~O13--O14 the observable-chain side is effectively closed on the current exact bridge: the remaining gap is no longer the existence of a floor but only how much of the present slack one wants to recover analytically.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O15 (Ratio-box \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} floor).}
The closed form of Proposition~O14 depends only on the two dimensionless ratios
\[
a:=\frac{\gamma_\phi}{\Delta E},
\qquad
b:=\frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}}{\Delta E}.
\]
Indeed
\[
C_{\max}=F(a,b),
\]
where
\[
F(a,b)
=
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{1+4b^2+4ab}}
\exp\!\left[
-\frac{a+2b}{\sqrt{1-a^2}}
\arctan\!\left(\frac{\sqrt{1-a^2}}{a+2b}\right)
\right].
\]
This representation is scale-free: the dependence on $\Delta E$ enters only through the ratios $(a,b)$.
On the exported exact bridge, \path{code/audit_open_system_cmax_ratio_floor_source.py} verifies that $F$ is monotone decreasing on the audited ratio box
\[
0\le a\le a_{\max},
\qquad
0\le b\le b_{\max},
\]
with derivative margins
\[
\max \partial_a F=-2.44\times10^{-1}<0,
\qquad
\max \partial_b F=-4.88\times10^{-1}<0.
\]
Here
\[
a_{\max}=\max_D \frac{\gamma_\phi(D)}{\Delta E(D)}
=2.06749\times10^{-4},
\qquad
b_{\max}=\max_D \frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\Delta E(D)}
=2.93097\times10^{-1}.
\]
Therefore the closed-form ratio floor is
\[
C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,closed)\ floor}=F(a_{\max},b_{\max})=2.34328\times10^{-1}.
\]
Subtracting the exported exact-vs-closed residual bound from Proposition~O14 gives the rigorous exact-bridge floor
\[
C_{\max}(D)\ge
C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}
:=
C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,closed)\ floor}
-\max_D |C_{\max}^{\rm(export)}-C_{\max}^{\rm(closed)}|
=2.34045\times10^{-1}.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O14 (Tightened \texorpdfstring{$\chi_{\rm eff}$}{chi_eff} floor from the ratio-box \texorpdfstring{$C_{\max}$}{Cmax} floor).}
Combining Proposition~O15 with Proposition~O13 yields
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)
\ge
A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)} C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}
=2.43752\times10^{-7}.
\]
The exact exported floors are
\[
C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}=2.34255\times10^{-1},
\qquad
\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}=9.03367\times10^{-7},
\]
so
\[
\frac{C_{\max}^{\rm exact\ floor}}{C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}}=1.000895,
\qquad
\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
{A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)} C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}}
=3.706.
\]
Thus the observable-chain side is not merely known to admit a floor: after Proposition~O15 the remaining gap is essentially only the slack in the amplitude factor $A_\chi^{\rm(sep\ floor)}$, not in the coherence-response floor itself.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O16 (Amplitude-floor alignment with the mix-rate floor on the exact bridge).}
On the exported exact bridge,
\[
A_\chi(D)=\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\gamma_{\rm ref}(D)}
=
\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\omega_1(D)^{11}}
=
\kappa_{\rm env}\frac{2g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)}{\omega_1(D)^{11}}.
\]
\path{code/audit_open_system_achi_amplitude_source.py} verifies the exact residuals
\[
\max_D \left|A_\chi-\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}}{\omega_1^{11}}\right|
=1.73\times10^{-16},
\qquad
\max_D \left|A_\chi-\kappa_{\rm env}\frac{2g_x^2S_{xx}}{\omega_1^{11}}\right|
=1.01\times10^{-16}.
\]
Moreover the three minima
\[
\arg\min_D A_\chi(D),
\qquad
\arg\min_D \gamma_{\rm mix}(D),
\qquad
\arg\min_D \frac{g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)}{\omega_1(D)^{11}}
\]
all occur at the same audited knot
\[
D=11.
\]
Hence the exact amplitude floor is already determined by the mix-rate floor together with the local first-mode scale at that same knot:
\[
A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}
=
\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm floor}}{\omega_1(D_{\gamma,\min})^{11}}
=1.81567\times10^{-6},
\]
with
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm floor}=5.22995\times10^{-4},
\qquad
\omega_1(D_{\gamma,\min})=1.78219.
\]
If one instead keeps the same numerator floor but replaces the local denominator by the global first-mode ceiling,
\[
A_\chi^{(\gamma,\omega{\rm ceil})}
:=
\frac{2\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm floor}}{(\omega_1^{\rm ceil})^{11}}
=1.10807\times10^{-6},
\qquad
\omega_1^{\rm ceil}=1.86402,
\]
then the entire remaining amplitude-side slack on the exported bridge is
\[
\frac{A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}}{A_\chi^{(\gamma,\omega{\rm ceil})}}
=
\left(\frac{\omega_1^{\rm ceil}}{\omega_1(D_{\gamma,\min})}\right)^{11}
=1.63858.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O15 (Tightened observable floor from the aligned amplitude source).}
Combining Proposition~O16 with Corollary~O14 yields, on the exported exact bridge,
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}(D)\ge
A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}\,C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}
=4.24949\times10^{-7}.
\]
The exact exported floor remains
\[
\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}=9.03367\times10^{-7},
\]
so
\[
\frac{\chi_{\rm eff}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
{A_\chi^{\rm exact\ floor}C_{\max}^{\rm(ratio,rig)\ floor}}
=2.12582.
\]
Thus after Propositions~O15--O16 the residual observable-chain slack is no longer spread over both the coherence-response and amplitude factors.  The coherence-response floor is already essentially saturated, and the remaining gap is concentrated in proving an a priori floor for $\gamma_{\rm mix}$ together with the corresponding local first-mode denominator control.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O17 (Alignment of the \texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm mix}$}{gamma_mix} floor with the \texorpdfstring{$x$}{x}-channel overlap floor on the exact bridge).}
On the exported exact bridge,
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)=\kappa_{\rm env}\,g_x(D)^2\,S_{xx}(D).
\]
\path{code/audit_open_system_gamma_mix_floor_alignment.py} verifies the exact reconstruction
\[
\max_D \left|\gamma_{\rm mix}-\kappa_{\rm env} g_x^2 S_{xx}\right|
=4.40\times10^{-14}.
\]
Moreover the minima of
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D),\qquad
g_x(D),\qquad
g_x(D)^2,\qquad
g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)
\]
all occur at the same audited knot
\[
D=11.
\]
Hence the exact mix-rate floor is already controlled by the $x$-channel overlap floor together with the local response factor at that same knot:
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm exact\ floor}
=
\kappa_{\rm env}\,(g_x^{\rm floor})^2\,S_{xx}(D_{g_x,\min})
=5.22995\times10^{-4},
\]
where
\[
g_x^{\rm floor}=3.35398\times10^{-5},
\qquad
S_{xx}(D_{g_x,\min})=1.07984.
\]
If one instead keeps the same overlap floor but replaces the local response factor by the global response minimum,
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}^{(g_x,S_{xx}^{\min})}
:=
\kappa_{\rm env}\,(g_x^{\rm floor})^2\,S_{xx}^{\rm floor}
=4.91560\times10^{-4},
\]
with
\[
S_{xx}^{\rm floor}=1.01493
\qquad\text{at }D=5,
\]
then the remaining floor slack is exactly
\[
\frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm exact\ floor}}
{\gamma_{\rm mix}^{(g_x,S_{xx}^{\min})}}
=
\frac{S_{xx}(D_{g_x,\min})}{S_{xx}^{\rm floor}}
=1.06395.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O16 (The remaining \texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm mix}$}{gamma_mix} slack is not response-dominated).}
Proposition~O17 shows that the exact exported \texorpdfstring{$\gamma_{\rm mix}$}{gamma_mix} floor is already aligned with the $x$-channel overlap floor, and that replacing the local response factor by the global response minimum changes the floor by only
\[
6.39\%.
\]
Therefore the next proof-side target should not be read as an independent floor theorem for $S_{xx}$.  On the current exact bridge the response factor is already mild and the dominant unresolved ingredient is the overlap-side floor itself, i.e. an a priori lower bound on $g_x$ (equivalently on $g_x^2S_{xx}$ up to this small response uplift).

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O18 (Parity-diagonal source for the \texorpdfstring{$g_x$}{gx} floor on the exact bridge).}
Let
\[
V_{\rm par}^{(\delta V)}(D)
=
\begin{pmatrix}
V_{11}(D) & V_{12}(D)\\
V_{12}(D) & V_{22}(D)
\end{pmatrix}
\]
be the parity-basis projection of the fluctuation operator $\delta V=V_{\rm eff}-\bar V(r)$ onto the lowest two parity modes, and let
\[
U_{LR}=\frac{1}{\sqrt 2}
\begin{pmatrix}
1&1\\
1&-1
\end{pmatrix}
\]
be the localized-basis rotation.  Then
\[
V_{\rm loc}^{(\delta V)}(D)
=
U_{LR}^{\top}V_{\rm par}^{(\delta V)}(D)U_{LR}
=
\begin{pmatrix}
\tfrac12(V_{11}+2V_{12}+V_{22}) & \tfrac12(V_{11}-V_{22})\\
\tfrac12(V_{11}-V_{22}) & \tfrac12(V_{11}-2V_{12}+V_{22})
\end{pmatrix}.
\]
Hence the localized-channel couplings are exact reparameterizations of the same projected fluctuation matrix:
\[
g_x(D)=\left|\bigl(V_{\rm loc}^{(\delta V)}(D)\bigr)_{LR}\right|
=
\frac12\left|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\right|,
\]
\[
g_z(D)=\frac12\left|\bigl(V_{\rm loc}^{(\delta V)}(D)\bigr)_{LL}-\bigl(V_{\rm loc}^{(\delta V)}(D)\bigr)_{RR}\right|
=
\left|V_{12}(D)\right|.
\]
\path{code/audit_open_system_gx_floor_source.py} verifies on the exported exact bridge that
\[
\max_D\left|g_x-\left|V_{LR}^{(\delta V)}\right|\right|=0,
\]
\[
\max_D\left|g_x-\frac12\left|V_{11}-V_{22}\right|\right|
=1.00\times10^{-16},
\qquad
\max_D\left|g_z-\left|V_{12}\right|\right|
=2.18\times10^{-18}.
\]
Moreover the minima of
\[
g_x(D),\qquad
\frac12\left|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\right|,\qquad
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)
\]
all occur at the same audited knot
\[
D=11,
\]
where
\[
g_x^{\rm floor}=3.35398\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\left|V_{11}-V_{22}\right|_{\rm floor}=6.70795\times10^{-5}.
\]
Thus the remaining exact-bridge floor question for $g_x$ is already equivalent to a static parity-diagonal contrast floor for the projected fluctuation operator $\delta V$.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O17 (The remaining \texorpdfstring{$g_x$}{gx} floor gap is static rather than bath-side).}
Propositions~O17--O18 reduce the last open-system overlap-side floor statement to the chain
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm exact\ floor}
=
\kappa_{\rm env}\,(g_x^{\rm floor})^2\,S_{xx}(D_{g_x,\min}),
\qquad
g_x^{\rm floor}
=
\frac12\left|V_{11}-V_{22}\right|_{\rm floor}.
\]
Therefore the unresolved proof-side target is no longer naturally read as a bath-response theorem for $S_{xx}$ or as an additional localized-basis theorem for $V_{LR}^{(\delta V)}$.  On the current exact bridge it is enough to produce an a priori lower bound on the parity-diagonal contrast
\[
\left|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\right|
\]
for the projected fluctuation operator $\delta V$ over the audited knot window.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O19 (One-center pair plus mirror remainder decomposition for the parity contrast).}
Write the two-center shifted potential as
\[
U_2(\rho,z;D)=U_+(\rho,z;D)+U_-(\rho,z;D)+U_\times(\rho,z;D),
\]
where
\[
U_\pm
=
m_0^2\bigl((1+s_\pm)^2-1\bigr)+c_\xi\frac{\ell_\pm}{1+s_\pm},
\qquad
c_\xi:=1-6\xi,
\]
with
\[
s_\pm(\rho,z;D)=\frac{a}{\sqrt{\rho^2+(z\mp D/2)^2+\varepsilon^2}},
\qquad
\ell_\pm(\rho,z;D)=
-3a\varepsilon^2\bigl(\rho^2+(z\mp D/2)^2+\varepsilon^2\bigr)^{-5/2},
\]
and
\[
U_\times
=
2m_0^2 s_+ s_-
+
c_\xi\left[
\frac{\ell_++\ell_-}{1+s_++s_-}
-\frac{\ell_+}{1+s_+}
-\frac{\ell_-}{1+s_-}
\right].
\]
Let
\[
\delta V_j(\rho,z;D):=U_j(\rho,z;D)-\bar U_j(r;D),
\qquad
r=\sqrt{\rho^2+z^2},
\]
with the same origin-centered spherical-average convention as the exact bridge, so that
\[
\delta V=\delta V_+ + \delta V_- + \delta V_\times.
\]
For the exact contrast density
\[
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D):=
2\pi\rho\bigl(\psi_1(\rho,z;D)^2-\psi_2(\rho,z;D)^2\bigr),
\]
the parity-diagonal contrast decomposes exactly as
\[
V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)
=
\int \mathcal C\,\delta V_+
+
\int \mathcal C\,\delta V_-
+
\int \mathcal C\,\delta V_\times.
\]
On the symmetry-adapted continuum pair one has
\[
\int \mathcal C\,\delta V_+
=
\int \mathcal C\,\delta V_-,
\]
so the exact contrast may be read as a one-center pair plus a mirror/cross remainder:
\[
V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)=2I_{\rm self}(D)+I_\times(D).
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_self_mirror_source.py} closes the additive split to machine precision,
\[
\max_D\|U_2-U_+-U_--U_\times\|_\infty
=8.88\times10^{-15},
\]
\[
\max_D\|\delta V-\delta V_+-\delta V_--\delta V_\times\|_\infty
=7.11\times10^{-15},
\]
and reconstructs the projected contrast with
\[
\max_D\left|(V_{11}-V_{22})-(I_++I_-+I_\times)\right|
=3.79\times10^{-18}.
\]
Across the full audited window \(D=4,\dots,20\), the one-center pair carries between
\[
97.74\%
\quad\text{and}\quad
99.833\%
\]
of the exact parity contrast in absolute value, while the mirror/cross remainder contributes only
\[
0.167\%
\quad\text{to}\quad
2.257\%.
\]
At the exact floor knot \(D=11\),
\[
V_{11}-V_{22}=6.70795\times10^{-5},
\]
\[
I_+ + I_- = 6.69675\times10^{-5},
\qquad
I_\times = 1.12022\times10^{-7},
\]
so the exact floor is already \(99.833\%\) saturated by the one-center pair, with the remainder reduced to a \(0.167\%\) correction.  The cross piece itself splits as
\[
I_\times=I_\times^{\rm mass}+I_\times^{\rm deriv},
\]
where at \(D=11\)
\[
I_\times^{\rm mass}=3.85528\times10^{-7},
\qquad
I_\times^{\rm deriv}=-2.73506\times10^{-7},
\]
showing that the small remainder comes from a partial cancellation inside the mirror term rather than from the failure of the one-center anchor.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O18 (The remaining \texorpdfstring{$|V_{11}-V_{22}|$}{|V11-V22|} gap is now a one-center floor problem with a small static remainder).}
Propositions~O18--O19 sharpen the last overlap-side target from a generic parity-contrast floor to a much narrower statement.  On the exact bridge,
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|
\]
is already overwhelmingly carried by the one-center pair \(2I_{\rm self}(D)\), while the mirror/cross remainder \(I_\times(D)\) keeps the same sign as the exact contrast and remains below \(2.26\%\) of it on every audited knot.  Therefore the next proof-side task should not be framed as a new bath-side or barrier-center theorem.  A theorem-level lower bound on the one-center pair \(2I_{\rm self}(D)\), together with a coarse control of the small static remainder \(I_\times(D)\), is enough to close the present exact-bridge gap for
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O20 (Core-window lower bound for the one-center pair on the exact bridge).}
For \(R>0\), let
\[
B_\pm(R;D):=\left\{(\rho,z):\rho^2+(z\mp D/2)^2\le R^2\right\},
\qquad
B_{\rm core}(R;D):=B_+(R;D)\cup B_-(R;D),
\]
and define
\[
I_{\rm self,pair}(D):=I_+(D)+I_-(D),
\]
\[
I_{\rm self,core}^{(R)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm core}(R;D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,
\bigl(\delta V_+(\rho,z;D)+\delta V_-(\rho,z;D)\bigr)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
I_{\rm self,tail}^{(R)}(D)
:=
I_{\rm self,pair}(D)-I_{\rm self,core}^{(R)}(D).
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_self_floor_source.py} scans
\[
R\in[1.20,1.40]
\]
in steps of \(0.01\) and identifies the optimal safe radius
\[
R_\ast=1.35,
\]
for which every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\) satisfies
\[
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,pair}(D),
\qquad
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,tail}^{(R_\ast)}(D)
=
-\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,pair}(D).
\]
Hence
\[
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
=
|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
-|I_{\rm self,tail}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|.
\]
Moreover the same audit gives the uniform tail-to-core control
\[
|I_{\rm self,tail}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
\le
\eta_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|,
\qquad
\eta_\ast=0.573731\ldots
\]
so that
\[
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|,
\qquad
\lambda_\ast:=1-\eta_\ast=0.426269\ldots
\]
on the full audited window. Since the core-window floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
=
1.33812\times10^{-4},
\]
this already yields the nontrivial lower bound
\[
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
\ge
5.70400\times10^{-5}.
\]
The exact one-center-pair floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
=
6.69675\times10^{-5}
\qquad\text{at }D=11,
\]
so the optimized core-window lower bound is only a factor
\[
1.174
\]
below the exact minimum.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O19 (The parity-contrast floor now follows from a one-center core floor plus a tiny same-sign remainder).}
Combine Proposition~O20 with Proposition~O19 and the sign information from the same decomposition audit. On the exact bridge one has
\[
\operatorname{sgn} I_\times(D)=\operatorname{sgn}(V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D))
\]
for every audited knot, together with
\[
\max_D \frac{|I_\times(D)|}{|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|}=2.2568\%.
\]
Therefore
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|
\ge
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
\ge
5.70400\times10^{-5}
\]
already on the exact bridge, without invoking any barrier-center WKB ansatz. Since
\[
\min_D |V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|=6.70795\times10^{-5},
\]
the remaining proof-side gap has become very narrow: produce an a priori lower bound on the one-center core contribution \(I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}\), and retain only a coarse signed control of the small mirror remainder \(I_\times\).

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O21 (Compact box-anchor lower bound for the one-center core on the exact bridge).}
Keep the same optimal core radius \(R_\ast=1.35\), and define the compact local box anchor
\[
B_{\rm box}(\rho_\#,z_\#;D)
:=
\left\{(\rho,z): \rho\le \rho_\#,\ |z\mp D/2|\le z_\#\right\}\cap B_{\rm core}(R_\ast;D),
\]
with the canonical audited choice
\[
\rho_\#=0.70,\qquad z_\#=1.21.
\]
Let
\[
I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm box}(\rho_\#,z_\#;D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,
\bigl(\delta V_+(\rho,z;D)+\delta V_-(\rho,z;D)\bigr)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
and
\[
I_{\rm self,shell}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)
:=
I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)-I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D).
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_self_core_anchor_source.py} scans
\[
\rho_\#\in[0.55,0.85],\qquad z_\#\in[1.05,1.30],
\]
in steps of \(0.01\) and finds a flat optimal plateau at \(z_\#=1.21\) with \(\rho_\#\in[0.69,0.74]\).  On the exact bridge the canonical box \((\rho_\#,z_\#)=(0.70,1.21)\) already satisfies
\[
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D),
\qquad
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,shell}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)
\]
for every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\). Therefore
\[
|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
\ge
|I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)|.
\]
The corresponding compact-anchor floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)|
=
1.32321\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the exact core-window floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
=
1.33812\times10^{-4}.
\]
So the compact box anchor already saturates the exact core-window minimum to within a factor
\[
1.01127.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O20 (The one-center core floor is already carried by a compact local anchor).}
Combining Proposition~O21 with Proposition~O20 gives
\[
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}(D)|
\ge
5.64043\times10^{-5}
\]
on the full audited window. The exact one-center-pair and total parity-contrast floors remain
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|=6.69675\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\min_D |V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|=6.70795\times10^{-5},
\]
so the remaining gap from the compact anchor to the exact exported minima is now only a factor \(1.187\) to \(1.189\).  This further tightens the proof target: it is enough to prove a lower bound on the compact one-center anchor \(I_{\rm self,anchor}^{(\rho_\#,z_\#)}\), while the rest of \(I_{\rm self,core}^{(R_\ast)}\) can remain a same-sign completion and \(I_\times\) only needs coarse signed remainder control.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O22 (The compact anchor is already controlled by a same-center static source plus a local mirror loss).}
Keep the canonical compact anchor
\[
B_{\rm anchor}(D):=B_{\rm box}(0.70,1.21;D).
\]
For compact display, write
\[
B_\pm^a(D):=B_\pm(D)\cap B_{\rm anchor}(D).
\]
Split the anchor integral into its same-center and mirror pieces:
\[
I_{\rm self,anchor}(D)=I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)+I_{\rm mir,anchor}(D),
\]
where
\[
I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)
:=
\int_{B_+^a(D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,\delta V_+(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz
+
\int_{B_-^a(D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,\delta V_-(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
I_{\rm mir,anchor}(D)
:=
\int_{B_+^a(D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,\delta V_-(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz
+
\int_{B_-^a(D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,\delta V_+(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_anchor_static_source.py} scans all same-center sub-boxes
\[
\rho_{\rm box}\in[0.40,0.70],\qquad
z_{\rm box}\in[0.80,1.21],
\]
in steps of \(0.01\), requiring the same-center box and the remaining same-center shell to keep the sign of \(I_{\rm self,anchor}\) on every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\).  Under this criterion the optimal box is the full canonical anchor itself:
\[
(\rho_{\rm box}^{\rm opt},z_{\rm box}^{\rm opt})=(0.69,1.21)\sim(0.70,1.21).
\]
Moreover, on the full audited window one has
\[
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm self,anchor}(D),
\]
and a uniform local mirror ratio
\[
\mu_{\rm anc}
:=
\max_D \frac{|I_{\rm mir,anchor}(D)|}{|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|}
=
0.415143\ldots<1.
\]
Therefore
\[
|I_{\rm self,anchor}(D)|
\ge
(1-\mu_{\rm anc})\,|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|.
\]
The same-center static floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|=1.63757\times10^{-4}
\qquad\text{at }D=11,
\]
so the induced anchor lower bound is
\[
|I_{\rm self,anchor}(D)|
\ge
(1-\mu_{\rm anc})\,|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
9.57744\times10^{-5}.
\]
Compared against the exact compact-anchor floor
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,anchor}(D)|=1.32321\times10^{-4},
\]
this static-source bound is only a factor
\[
1.38159
\]
below the exact minimum.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O21 (The remaining overlap-side gap has been reduced to a same-center static anchor source).}
Combining Proposition~O22 with Corollary~O20 gives
\[
|I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast\,|I_{\rm self,anchor}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_\ast(1-\mu_{\rm anc})\,|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
4.08256\times10^{-5}.
\]
The exact one-center-pair and total parity-contrast floors remain
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm self,pair}(D)|=6.69675\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\min_D |V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|=6.70795\times10^{-5},
\]
so the residual gap from the audited exact minima is now only a factor
\[
1.640\text{ to }1.643.
\]
This isolates the true proof-side target even further: the overlap-side problem is no longer to control barrier-center WKB tails, nor even the full compact anchor, but to produce an a priori lower bound on the same-center static source \(I_{\rm diag,anchor}\).  The compact anchor itself is already obtained from that source by a local mirror correction with uniform ratio \(\mu_{\rm anc}<0.42\), while \(I_\times\) stays only a coarse same-sign remainder in the total parity contrast.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O23 (Strict same-center subboxes do not improve the static anchor floor).}
To test whether the proof target can be localized further, define a strict same-center subbox inside the canonical anchor by
\[
B_{\rm diag,box}(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box;D)
:=
\left\{(\rho,z):\rho\le \rho_\Box,\ |z\mp D/2|\le \zeta_\Box\right\}
\subsetneq B_{\rm anchor}(D),
\]
with at least one strict inequality
\[
\rho_\Box<0.70\qquad\text{or}\qquad \zeta_\Box<1.21.
\]
Let
\[
I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)
=
I_{\rm diag,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
+
I_{\rm diag,shell}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D).
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_box_source.py} scans
\[
\rho_\Box\in[0.40,0.70],\qquad
\zeta_\Box\in[0.80,1.21],
\]
in steps of \(0.01\), excluding the trivial full-anchor choice. On every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\), the optimal strict plateau occurs at
\[
\rho_\Box\in[0.63,0.68],\qquad \zeta_\Box=1.21,
\]
and the rounded representative choice
\[
(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)=(0.65,1.21)
\]
already satisfies
\[
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm diag,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D),
\qquad
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm diag,shell}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn} I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)
\]
for every audited knot. Therefore
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
|I_{\rm diag,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)|.
\]
The resulting strict-box floor is
\[
\min_D \left|I_{\rm diag,box}^{(0.65,1.21)}(D)\right|
=
1.48301\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the exact same-center-anchor floor is
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
=
1.63757\times10^{-4}.
\]
So even after forcing a genuine strict localization, the loss is only a factor
\[
1.10422.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O22 (The proof target has stabilized at the full same-center anchor scale).}
Proposition~O23 shows that no genuinely smaller same-center box inside the canonical anchor improves the static-source floor on the audited bridge. The best strict local box already carries
\[
\frac{1}{1.10422}=90.56\%
\]
of the exact same-center-anchor floor. Thus the next theorem step should not be to keep shrinking the support, but to lower-bound \(I_{\rm diag,anchor}\) itself. In other words, the overlap-side mainline has stabilized:
\[
I_{\rm diag,anchor}
\]
is now the natural one-center static source to target, while \(I_{\rm mir,anchor}\) remains only a coarse local mirror correction and \(I_\times\) remains only a coarse same-sign remainder in the full parity contrast.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O24 (Oriented moment reduction of the same-center static anchor).}
Keep the canonical same-center static anchor \(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)\) and define the oriented anchor density
\[
q_{\rm diag}(\rho,z;D)
:=
\operatorname{sgn}\!\bigl(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)\bigr)\,
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\Bigl(
\delta V_+(\rho,z;D)\,\mathbf 1_{B_+^a(D)}
+
\delta V_-(\rho,z;D)\,\mathbf 1_{B_-^a(D)}
\Bigr).
\]
Let
\[
P_{\rm diag}(D):=\int [q_{\rm diag}(\rho,z;D)]_+\,d\rho\,dz,
\qquad
N_{\rm diag}(D):=\int [q_{\rm diag}(\rho,z;D)]_-\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
so that
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|=P_{\rm diag}(D)-N_{\rm diag}(D).
\]
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_moment_source.py} closes this split to machine precision,
\[
\max_D \Bigl||I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|-(P_{\rm diag}(D)-N_{\rm diag}(D))\Bigr|
=
6.51\times10^{-19},
\]
and gives the uniform moment ratio
\[
\nu_{\rm diag}^{(\pm)}
:=
\max_D \frac{N_{\rm diag}(D)}{P_{\rm diag}(D)}
=
0.911532\ldots
\quad\text{at }D=11.
\]
Therefore
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
\bigl(1-\nu_{\rm diag}^{(\pm)}\bigr)\,P_{\rm diag}(D).
\]
The positive-moment floor itself is
\[
\min_D P_{\rm diag}(D)=1.17067\times10^{-3}
\qquad\text{at }D=20,
\]
so the induced intrinsic static lower bound is
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
\bigl(1-\nu_{\rm diag}^{(\pm)}\bigr)\,\min_D P_{\rm diag}(D)
\ge
1.03567\times10^{-4}.
\]
Compared against the exact same-center-anchor floor
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|=1.65556\times10^{-4},
\]
this oriented-moment bound is only a factor
\[
1.59854
\]
below the exact minimum, while remaining more intrinsic than any support-based subbox choice.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O23 (The remaining anchor-side gap is now cancellation control, not support selection).}
Proposition~O24 gives a direct lower bound on the full same-center anchor source without introducing any new support cutoff:
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
\ge
1.03567\times10^{-4}.
\]
This is stronger than the previous mass-only route
\[
8.17293\times10^{-5}
\]
by a factor
\[
1.26719,
\]
and it is also slightly stronger than the induced compact-anchor lower bound
\[
9.57744\times10^{-5}
\]
coming from Proposition~O22, by a factor
\[
1.08136.
\]
So the proof-side interpretation is now sharper still: the obstacle is no longer where to localize the source. The support has already stabilized at the full same-center anchor scale, and the remaining loss is dominated by the positive/negative cancellation inside that anchor.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O25 (Shell-isolated cancellation control for the same-center anchor).}
To separate genuine interior cancellation from mere support leakage, fix a same-center axial slab cut
\[
\zeta_{\rm cut}<1.21
\]
and write the canonical anchor as
\[
B_{\rm anchor}(D)=B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)\sqcup B_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D),
\]
where
\[
B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)
:=
\bigl\{(\rho,z)\in B_{\rm anchor}(D): |z\mp D/2|\le \zeta_{\rm cut}\bigr\}.
\]
Define the corresponding inner and outer oriented moments
\[
P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)} [q_{\rm diag}]_+\,d\rho\,dz,
\qquad
N_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)} [q_{\rm diag}]_-\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
P_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)} [q_{\rm diag}]_+\,d\rho\,dz,
\qquad
N_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)} [q_{\rm diag}]_-\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Then
\[
\nu_{\rm diag}^{(\pm)}(D)
:=
\frac{N_{\rm diag}(D)}{P_{\rm diag}(D)}
\le
\frac{N_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)}{P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)}
+
\frac{N_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)}{P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut})}(D)},
\]
because \(N_{\rm diag}=N_{\rm in}+N_{\rm out}\) and \(P_{\rm diag}\ge P_{\rm in}\).
The audit \path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_cancellation_source.py}
scans the nontrivial slab family
\[
\zeta_{\rm cut}\in[0.80,1.20]
\]
and finds the optimal shell-isolating choice
\[
\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast}=1.20.
\]
On the full audited window this gives
\[
\min_D P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)=1.17067\times10^{-3},
\]
\[
\max_D \frac{N_{\rm out}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)}{P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)}
=
8.68193\times10^{-3},
\]
\[
\max_D \frac{N_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)}{P_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)}
=
0.912743\ldots
\]
and therefore the full cancellation ratio obeys the explicit audited bound
\[
\nu_{\rm diag}^{(\pm)}(D)
\le
0.912743 + 8.68193\times10^{-3}
\le
0.921425
\]
for every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\). The shell leakage is thus below one percent at the level of the normalized cancellation budget.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O24 (The anchor-side cancellation problem is interior and mass-dominated).}
The same audit further decomposes the oriented anchor density as
\[
q_{\rm diag}=q_{\rm mass}+q_{\rm der},
\]
where \(q_{\rm mass}\) and \(q_{\rm der}\) are the same-center mass and derivative contributions built from
\[
U_{\pm}^{\rm mass}=m_0^2(\Omega_\pm^2-1),
\qquad
U_{\pm}^{\rm der}=(1-6\xi)\frac{\nabla^2\Omega_\pm}{\Omega_\pm},
\]
after the same origin-centered spherical-average subtraction. Writing the corresponding positive/negative moments as
\[
P_{\rm mass},\,N_{\rm mass},\,P_{\rm der},\,N_{\rm der},
\]
the audited exact bridge satisfies
\[
\min_D \frac{P_{\rm mass}(D)}{P_{\rm diag}(D)}=1.00507,
\qquad
\max_D \frac{P_{\rm der}(D)}{P_{\rm diag}(D)}=8.53082\times10^{-2},
\]
\[
\min_D \frac{N_{\rm mass}(D)}{N_{\rm diag}(D)}=0.712802,
\qquad
\max_D \frac{N_{\rm der}(D)}{N_{\rm diag}(D)}=0.570882.
\]
So the shell part is negligible, the positive carrier is already overwhelmingly same-center mass-sector, and the remaining adverse budget stays interior to the one-center anchor rather than coming from support selection or mirror leakage. The proof-side target has therefore narrowed again: the remaining overlap-side gap is best read as a one-center interior cancellation bound, not as a localization problem.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O26 (Interior mass-sector cancellation controls the floor knot).}
Keep the same canonical same-center anchor and the shell-isolating cut
\[
\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast}=1.20.
\]
On the resulting inner slab \(B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)\), define the oriented same-center mass contribution
\[
I_{\rm mass,in}(D)
:=
\operatorname{sgn}\!\bigl(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)\bigr)
\int_{B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)} q_{\rm mass}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
with corresponding positive/negative moments
\[
P_{\rm mass,in}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_+\,d\rho\,dz,
\qquad
N_{\rm mass,in}(D):=\int_{B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Then
\[
I_{\rm mass,in}(D)=P_{\rm mass,in}(D)-N_{\rm mass,in}(D)
=
\bigl(1-\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)\bigr)P_{\rm mass,in}(D),
\]
where
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
:=
\frac{N_{\rm mass,in}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}.
\]
Writing the full oriented anchor as
\[
|I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
=
I_{\rm mass,in}(D)+I_{\rm der,in}(D)+I_{\rm out}(D),
\]
with \(I_{\rm der,in}\) the derivative-sector correction on the same inner slab and \(I_{\rm out}\) the residual oriented shell term, the audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_cancellation_source.py}
shows that the same shell-isolating cut remains optimal for the mass-net floor:
\[
\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast}=1.20.
\]
At that cut the exact bridge satisfies
\[
I_{\rm mass,in}(D)\ge 0
\quad\text{for all audited }D,
\]
and the extrema align on the same knot:
\[
\arg\min_D |I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|
=
\arg\min_D I_{\rm mass,in}(D)
=
\arg\max_D \nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
=
11.
\]
Numerically,
\[
\min_D |I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D)|=1.65556\times10^{-4},
\]
\[
\min_D I_{\rm mass,in}(D)=7.92440\times10^{-5},
\qquad
\max_D \nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)=0.957812.
\]
At the floor knot \(D=11\), one furthermore finds
\[
I_{\rm der,in}(11)=8.38290\times10^{-5}>0,
\qquad
I_{\rm out}(11)=2.48275\times10^{-6}>0,
\]
so the derivative and shell pieces are non-adverse there and the exact floor is obtained by adding a positive derivative uplift and a tiny positive shell correction to the interior mass-sector net.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O25 (The adverse cancellation is set by the interior one-center mass sector).}
Proposition~O26 isolates the last overlap-side obstruction more sharply than Proposition~O25. The floor knot is not selected by shell leakage, by mirror leakage, or by any smaller support cutoff. It is selected by the same interior knot that maximizes the one-center mass-sector cancellation ratio
\(\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}\), while the derivative and shell terms are already non-adverse there. In particular, the exact floor lies only a factor
\[
\frac{1.65556\times10^{-4}}{7.92440\times10^{-5}}
=
2.08919
\]
above the interior mass-sector net, and the gap is accounted for by a positive derivative uplift at the very same knot rather than by any new adverse mechanism. So the proof-side target has narrowed again: the remaining overlap-side gap is best read as an interior one-center mass-sector cancellation problem, with derivative and shell pieces retained only as signed local corrections.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O27 (The positive mass-carrier floor survives a strict axial trim).}
Keep the same inner slab
\[
B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D),
\qquad
\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast}=1.20,
\]
and define for any same-center subbox
\[
B_{\rm mass,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
:=
\bigl\{(\rho,z)\in B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D): \rho\le \rho_\Box,\ |z\mp D/2|\le \zeta_\Box\bigr\}
\]
the positive mass-carrier moment
\[
P_{\rm mass,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm mass,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_+\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Since \([q_{\rm mass}]_+\ge 0\), monotonicity gives the exact lower bound
\[
P_{\rm mass,in}(D)\ge P_{\rm mass,box}^{(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)}(D)
\]
for every such subbox.
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_positive_source.py}
scans
\[
\rho_\Box\in[0.35,0.70],
\qquad
\zeta_\Box\in[0.70,1.20],
\]
and finds that the optimal strict positive-carrier plateau already sits on the near-full axial trims
\[
\rho_\Box\in[0.69,0.70],
\qquad
\zeta_\Box\in[1.10,1.19].
\]
Using the canonical strict representative
\[
(\rho_\Box,\zeta_\Box)=(0.70,1.19),
\]
one gets
\[
\min_D P_{\rm mass,box}^{(0.70,1.19)}(D)
=
\min_D P_{\rm mass,in}(D)
=
1.25520\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=20\). Moreover, on the full audited window
\[
\min_D
\frac{P_{\rm mass,box}^{(0.70,1.19)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
0.999825
\]
at \(D=11\). So the positive mass carrier is already fully stabilized on a strict axial trim of the canonical one-center slab.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O26 (The positive-carrier side is no longer the blocker).}
Propositions~O26 and O27 now split the interior mass-sector problem cleanly in two:
\[
I_{\rm mass,in}
=
\bigl(1-\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}\bigr)P_{\rm mass,in},
\qquad
P_{\rm mass,in}\ge P_{\rm mass,box}^{(0.70,1.19)}.
\]
The positive carrier floor is therefore already stable under a strict one-center axial trim, with no loss at the audited floor knot and at most
\[
1-0.999825=1.75\times10^{-4}
\]
relative loss anywhere on the audited window. The remaining overlap-side gap is thus no longer a positive-carrier problem; it is the interior one-center mass-sector cancellation ratio itself.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O28 (The adverse mass carrier localizes to an off-axis one-center annulus).}
Keep the same canonical inner slab
\[
B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D),
\qquad
\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast}=1.20,
\]
and define for any annular trim
\[
A_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
:=
\bigl\{(\rho,z)\in B_{\rm in}^{(\zeta_{\rm cut}^{\ast})}(D):
\rho_{\min}\le \rho\le 0.70,\ |z\mp D/2|\le \zeta_{\Box}\bigr\}
\]
the off-axis adverse-carrier moment
\[
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
:=
\int_{A_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Since \([q_{\rm mass}]_-\ge 0\), one has the exact split
\[
N_{\rm mass,in}(D)
=
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
+
N_{\rm mass,tail}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D),
\]
with
\[
N_{\rm mass,tail}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)\ge 0.
\]
Therefore
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
=
\frac{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
\frac{N_{\rm mass,tail}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_negative_source.py}
scans
\[
\rho_{\min}\in[0.03,0.30],
\qquad
\zeta_{\Box}\in[1.10,1.20].
\]
If one allows arbitrary strict trims, the optimum is a near-full slab. Constraining instead to genuinely off-axis annuli with \(\rho_{\min}\ge 0.12\), the best plateau sits at
\[
\rho_{\min}\in[0.12,0.15],
\qquad
\zeta_{\Box}=1.20.
\]
Using the canonical representative
\[
(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})=(0.15,1.20),
\]
one gets
\[
\min_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}{N_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
0.949763
\]
at \(D=11\), while the axial-core leakage obeys
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,tail}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
4.81178\times10^{-2}
\]
again at \(D=11\). The same knot also maximizes the annular adverse load,
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
0.909694.
\]
So the adverse-carrier side is already stabilized at an off-axis one-center annulus, with only a small axial-core leakage left outside it.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O27 (The remaining cancellation gap is an off-axis adverse-annulus problem).}
Combining Propositions~O26--O28 gives
\[
I_{\rm mass,in}
=
\bigl(1-\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}\bigr)P_{\rm mass,in},
\qquad
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
\le
\frac{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\]
At the exact floor knot \(D=11\), this reads
\[
0.957812
=
0.909694 + 4.81178\times10^{-2},
\]
so the entire mass-sector cancellation gap is already explained by an off-axis one-center adverse annulus plus a small axial-core leakage. In particular, the positive-carrier side is no longer the issue, and neither shell leakage nor mirror leakage sets the overlap-side floor. The remaining proof-side target has narrowed once more: it is now enough to control the adverse annular carrier \(N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}\), with the axial core retained only as a small corrective leakage.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O29 (The adverse annulus floor survives a strict axial trim).}
Keep the canonical adverse annulus
\[
A_{\rm neg}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
\]
from Proposition~O28 and define for any strict annular trim
\[
A_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
\subsetneq
A_{\rm neg}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D),
\qquad
\rho_{\min}\ge 0.15,\ \zeta_{\Box}\le 1.20,
\]
the trimmed adverse-carrier moment
\[
N_{\rm mass,subann}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
:=
\int_{A_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Because \([q_{\rm mass}]_-\ge 0\), one has the exact monotone split
\[
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
=
N_{\rm mass,subann}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)
+
N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D),
\qquad
N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(\rho_{\min},\zeta_{\Box})}(D)\ge 0.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_annulus_source.py}
scans
\[
\rho_{\min}\in[0.15,0.30],
\qquad
\zeta_{\Box}\in[1.10,1.20]
\]
inside the canonical annulus and finds that the optimal strict plateau already lies at the smallest possible radial trim,
\[
\rho_{\min}=0.15,
\qquad
\zeta_{\Box}=1.19.
\]
Using this canonical strict representative, one gets
\[
\min_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,subann}^{(0.15,1.19)}(D)}
{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}
=
0.984061
\]
at \(D=16\), while the omitted cap leakage satisfies
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(0.15,1.19)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
7.47726\times10^{-3}
\]
again at \(D=16\). The trimmed adverse-annulus floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,subann}^{(0.15,1.19)}(D)
=
7.64028\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the full annulus floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
=
7.73394\times10^{-4},
\]
so the strict trim lowers the audited floor by only the factor
\[
\frac{7.73394\times10^{-4}}{7.64028\times10^{-4}}
=
1.01226.
\]
Thus the adverse annular carrier is already stable at the canonical full-annulus scale: strict axial trims do not produce a meaningfully stronger theorem object.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O28 (The proof target stabilizes at the full adverse-annulus scale).}
Propositions~O28 and O29 together imply that the support side of the adverse carrier is now exhausted:
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
\le
\frac{N_{\rm mass,subann}^{(0.15,1.19)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}
+
7.47726\times10^{-3},
\]
but the extra strict-trim leakage is already an order of magnitude smaller than the axial-core leakage from Proposition~O28. So the remaining proof-side gap is no longer about how to choose the annular support: it is the adverse magnitude carried by the canonical annulus itself. In particular, the natural theorem object has stabilized at
\[
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)},
\]
with only tiny axial and cap leakages retained as corrective constants.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O30 (The adverse annulus magnitude localizes to a radial shoulder).}
Keep the canonical adverse annulus \(A_{\rm neg}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)\) and, for any strict radial trim
\[
\rho_{\rm sh}>0.15,
\]
define the adverse shoulder
\[
S_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)
:=
\{(\rho,z)\in A_{\rm neg}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D): \rho\ge \rho_{\rm sh}\},
\]
with shoulder and rim moments
\[
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)
:=
\int_{S_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
N_{\rm mass,rim}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)
:=
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
-
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D).
\]
Because \([q_{\rm mass}]_-\ge 0\), one has the exact monotone split
\[
N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
=
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)
+
N_{\rm mass,rim}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D),
\qquad
N_{\rm mass,rim}^{(\rho_{\rm sh},1.20)}(D)\ge 0.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_shoulder_source.py}
scans strict radial trims
\[
\rho_{\rm sh}\in[0.16,0.30]
\]
inside the canonical annulus and finds a flat optimal plateau
\[
\rho_{\rm sh}\in[0.16,0.20].
\]
Using the clean canonical representative
\[
\rho_{\rm sh}=0.20,
\]
one gets
\[
\min_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}
{N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}
=
0.940824
\]
at \(D=11\), while the discarded inner-rim leakage obeys
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,rim}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
5.38319\times10^{-2}
\]
again at \(D=11\). The shoulder floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the full adverse-annulus floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,ann}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)
=
7.73394\times10^{-4},
\]
so the radial shoulder loses only the factor
\[
\frac{7.73394\times10^{-4}}{7.37447\times10^{-4}}
=
1.04874.
\]
Thus the adverse magnitude is already carried almost entirely by an off-axis radial shoulder: the inner rim is only a secondary leakage.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O29 (The cancellation gap narrows to an adverse shoulder plus two small leakages).}
Combining Proposition~O30 with Proposition~O28 yields
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
\le
\frac{N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
5.38319\times10^{-2}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\]
At the exact worst cancellation knot \(D=11\), this reads
\[
0.957812
=
0.855863
+
5.38319\times10^{-2}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\]
So the positive carrier, the axial core, and the inner radial rim are all now demoted to corrective terms. The remaining proof-side target has narrowed again: it is enough to control the off-axis one-center adverse shoulder
\[
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)},
\]
with only the small axial-core and inner-rim leakages retained outside it.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O31 (The adverse shoulder floor survives a genuine axial hollow).}
Keep the canonical adverse shoulder
\[
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)
\]
from Proposition~O30 and define, for any axial hollow
\[
\zeta_{\rm low}>0,
\]
the adverse shoulder band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)
:=
\{(\rho,z)\in S_{\rm neg}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D): \zeta_{\rm low}\le |z\mp D/2|\le 1.20\},
\]
with band and shoulder-core moments
\[
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
N_{\rm mass,core}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)
:=
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)
-
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D).
\]
Because \([q_{\rm mass}]_-\ge 0\), one has the exact monotone split
\[
N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)
=
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)
+
N_{\rm mass,core}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D),
\qquad
N_{\rm mass,core}^{(0.20;\zeta_{\rm low},1.20)}(D)\ge 0.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_shoulder_band_source.py}
scans genuine hollows
\[
\zeta_{\rm low}\in[0.10,0.40]
\]
inside the canonical shoulder and finds that the best hollow already sits at the smallest nontrivial cut,
\[
\zeta_{\rm low}=0.10.
\]
For this canonical representative one gets
\[
\min_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}
=
0.951019
\]
at \(D=11\), while the discarded shoulder-core leakage satisfies
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,core}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
4.19214\times10^{-2}
\]
again at \(D=11\). The adverse-band floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
exactly matching the canonical shoulder floor
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
so the audited factor
\[
\frac{\min_D N_{\rm mass,shoulder}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}
{\min_D N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
=
1
\]
to machine precision. Thus the adverse shoulder magnitude is already carried by a genuinely hollow off-axis band: the shoulder-core piece is only a small corrective leakage.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O30 (The cancellation gap narrows to an adverse band plus three small leakages).}
Combining Proposition~O31 with Corollary~O29 gives
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
\le
\frac{N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
4.19214\times10^{-2}
+
5.38319\times10^{-2}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\]
At the exact worst cancellation knot \(D=11\), this reads
\[
0.957812
=
0.813941
+
4.19214\times10^{-2}
+
5.38319\times10^{-2}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O32 (Strict corner trims do not improve the adverse-band floor).}
Keep the canonical adverse band
\[
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
from Proposition~O31 and define, for any strict corner trim
\[
