\rho_{\rm corner}>0.20,
\qquad
\zeta_{\rm corner}\ge 0.10,
\]
the corner subband
\[
C_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)
:=
\{(\rho,z)\in B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D): \rho\ge \rho_{\rm corner},\ \zeta_{\rm corner}\le |z\mp D/2|\le 1.20\},
\]
with corner and cap moments
\[
N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)
:=
\int_{C_{\rm neg}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)} [q_{\rm mass}]_-\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)
:=
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
-
N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D).
\]
Because \([q_{\rm mass}]_-\ge 0\), one has the exact monotone split
\[
\begin{aligned}
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
&=
N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)\\
&\quad+
N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D),\\
N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(\rho_{\rm corner};\zeta_{\rm corner},1.20)}(D)&\ge 0.
\end{aligned}
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_band_corner_source.py}
scans genuinely strict corner trims
\[
\rho_{\rm corner}\in[0.22,0.30],
\qquad
\zeta_{\rm corner}\in[0.10,0.40],
\]
inside the canonical adverse band and finds the best plateau
\[
\rho_{\rm corner}\in[0.22,0.27],
\qquad
\zeta_{\rm corner}=0.10.
\]
Using the clean canonical representative
\[
(\rho_{\rm corner},\zeta_{\rm corner})=(0.22,0.10),
\]
one gets
\[
\min_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(0.22;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
=
0.921342
\]
at \(D=4\), while the discarded cap leakage obeys
\[
\max_D
\frac{N_{\rm mass,cap}^{(0.22;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
=
6.35145\times10^{-2}
\]
at \(D=11\). The strict-corner floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(0.22;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
6.88668\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the canonical adverse-band floor is
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
so every genuinely strict corner trim loses the audited factor
\[
\frac{7.37447\times10^{-4}}{6.88668\times10^{-4}}
=
1.07083.
\]
Thus support-side localization is already exhausted at the full adverse-band scale: a strict corner trim does not produce a better theorem object.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O31 (The cancellation gap stabilizes at the full adverse-band scale).}
Combining Proposition~O32 with Corollary~O30 gives
\[
\begin{aligned}
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
&\le
\frac{N_{\rm mass,corner}^{(0.22;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+6.35145\times10^{-2}
+4.19214\times10^{-2}\\
&\quad
+5.38319\times10^{-2}
+4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\end{aligned}
\]
At the exact worst cancellation knot \(D=11\), this reads
\[
\begin{aligned}
0.957812
&=
0.750427
+6.35145\times10^{-2}
+4.19214\times10^{-2}\\
&\quad
+5.38319\times10^{-2}
+4.81178\times10^{-2}.
\end{aligned}
\]
So the dominant adverse band has now been tested against a genuinely strict corner localization and survives as the stable proof-side target: the remaining gap is best read as the full canonical adverse band plus only small cap, shoulder-core, inner-rim, and axial-core leakages.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O33 (The canonical adverse-band magnitude factorizes into a positive static kernel and a negative carrier moment).}
Keep the canonical adverse band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
from Proposition~O31. Define the one-center local mass kernel
\[
K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}(\rho,z;D)
:=
\bigl(U_+^{\rm mass}-\bar U_+^{\rm mass}\bigr)\mathbf 1_{B_+}
+
\bigl(U_-^{\rm mass}-\bar U_-^{\rm mass}\bigr)\mathbf 1_{B_-},
\]
restricted to \(B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)\), and the negative carrier density
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)
:=
\bigl[-\operatorname{sgn}(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D))\,\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\bigr]_+
\mathbf 1_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}.
\]
Then on the canonical band one has the exact pointwise factorization
\[
[q_{\rm mass}]_-(\rho,z;D)
=
K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}(\rho,z;D)\,W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D),
\]
provided \(K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}>0\) there. The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_mass_band_magnitude_source.py}
verifies precisely this structure across all audited knots \(D=4,\dots,20\):
\[
\max_D \|[q_{\rm mass}]_- - K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}W_{\rm band}^{(-)}\|_\infty = 0,
\]
and
\[
\min_D \inf_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}
=
1.82852\times10^{-2}
\]
at \(D=4\), so the kernel stays strictly positive on the whole canonical band. Writing the negative carrier moment as
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
and the weighted kernel average as
\[
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\frac{N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
{W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)},
\]
the audit finds
\[
5.38460\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
1.00728\times10^{-1}
\]
for all audited \(D\), with the minimum at \(D=5\) and the maximum at \(D=18\). The negative-carrier floor is
\[
\min_D W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
8.40086\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=20\), while the crude kernel-floor product
\[
\inf_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}\cdot W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\]
has audited floor
\[
4.28523\times10^{-4}
\]
at \(D=20\). Thus the adverse-band magnitude is no longer a black-box carrier: it is a positive one-center mass kernel against a negative carrier moment.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O32 (The remaining adverse-band proof target is the negative carrier moment).}
By Proposition~O33, the canonical adverse-band magnitude satisfies the exact identity
\[
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D),
\]
with
\[
5.38460\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
1.00728\times10^{-1}.
\]
Equivalently,
\[
\inf_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}\cdot W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\le
\sup_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}K_{\rm band}^{\rm mass}\cdot W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D).
\]
The audited band floor remains
\[
\min_D N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
whereas the kernel-floor product has floor
\[
4.28523\times10^{-4},
\]
so the exact band floor exceeds the coarse product floor by the factor
\[
\frac{7.37447\times10^{-4}}{4.28523\times10^{-4}}
=
1.72090.
\]
Therefore the support problem is already closed and the magnitude problem has narrowed again: to control the canonical adverse band, it is now enough to control the negative carrier moment \(W_{\rm band}^{(-)}\), with the positive one-center mass kernel confined to a narrow audited interval.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O34 (The negative carrier moment factorizes through an almost constant radial weight).}
Keep the canonical adverse band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
and define the negative overlap-band density
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)
:=
\bigl[-\operatorname{sgn}(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D))(\psi_1(\rho,z;D)^2-\psi_2(\rho,z;D)^2)\bigr]_+
\mathbf 1_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}.
\]
Since
\[
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)=2\pi\rho\bigl(\psi_1(\rho,z;D)^2-\psi_2(\rho,z;D)^2\bigr),
\]
the canonical negative carrier moment satisfies the exact pointwise identity
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)
=
2\pi\rho\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D).
\]
Integrating over the canonical band gives
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
2\pi\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} \rho\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Define the unweighted overlap-band moment
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
and the weighted radial factor
\[
\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\frac{W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)}{2\pi\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)}.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_band_negative_carrier_source.py}
verifies the exact factorization
\[
\max_D \|W_{\rm band}^{(-)}-2\pi\rho\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}\|_\infty
=
1.35525\times10^{-20},
\]
and finds the weighted radial factor confined to the tiny interval
\[
0.4434097
\le
\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
0.4438634,
\]
with the minimum at \(D=7\) and the maximum at \(D=6\). Its relative spread is only
\[
\frac{\max \bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}-\min \bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}}{\operatorname{mean}\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}}
=
1.02267\times10^{-3}.
\]
The canonical negative-carrier floor is
\[
\min_D W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
8.40086\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=20\), and the unweighted overlap-band floor is
\[
\min_D M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
3.01270\times10^{-3}
\]
again at \(D=20\). Therefore the negative carrier moment itself is already equivalent, up to an almost constant radial factor, to an unweighted overlap-band moment.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O33 (The remaining carrier-side proof target is the unweighted negative overlap-band moment).}
By Proposition~O34, one has the exact identity
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
2\pi\,\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D),
\]
with
\[
0.4434097
\le
\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
0.4438634.
\]
Equivalently,
\[
2\pi\,(0.4434097)\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
2\pi\,(0.4438634)\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D).
\]
At the audited floor knot \(D=20\), this reads
\[
W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(20)
=
2\pi\,(0.4438002)\,M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(20).
\]
Moreover,
\[
\frac{\min_D W_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)}
{2\pi(\min_D \bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D))(\min_D M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D))}
=
1.00088,
\]
so even the coarsest audited lower product is already essentially exact. Thus the carrier-side magnitude problem narrows once more: to control \(W_{\rm band}^{(-)}\), it is enough to control the unweighted negative overlap-band moment \(M_{\rm band}^{(-)}\), with only an almost constant radial factor left outside it.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O35 (The negative overlap-band moment is an absolute overlap-band mass times a signed-imbalance factor).}
Keep the canonical adverse band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
and define the unweighted signed overlap-band density
\[
m_{\rm band}(\rho,z;D)
:=
\operatorname{sgn}(I_{\rm diag,anchor}(D))\bigl(\psi_1(\rho,z;D)^2-\psi_2(\rho,z;D)^2\bigr)
\mathbf 1_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}.
\]
Define its negative moment, absolute moment, and signed residue by
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} [ -m_{\rm band}(\rho,z;D)]_+\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} |m_{\rm band}(\rho,z;D)|\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
R_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} m_{\rm band}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Since \(x_-=(|x|-x)/2\), one has the exact identity
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
\frac{M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)-R_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)}{2}.
\]
Equivalently, if
\[
r_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)
:=
\frac{R_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)}{M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)},
\qquad
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
:=
\frac{1-r_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)}{2},
\]
then
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)=\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D).
\]
Moreover, using the localized basis
\[
\phi_R=\frac{\psi_1+\psi_2}{\sqrt2},
\qquad
\phi_L=\frac{\psi_1-\psi_2}{\sqrt2},
\]
the parity-contrast identity
\[
\psi_1^2-\psi_2^2=2\phi_R\phi_L
\]
implies
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
2\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} |\phi_R(\rho,z;D)\phi_L(\rho,z;D)|\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_band_overlap_moment_source.py}
verifies both identities to machine precision:
\[
\max_D \left|M_{\rm band}^{(-)}-\frac{M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}-R_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}}{2}\right|
=
1.73472\times10^{-18},
\]
\[
\max_D \left|M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}-2\int |\phi_R\phi_L|\right|
=
3.46945\times10^{-18}.
\]
The signed-imbalance ratio satisfies
\[
-2.03788\times10^{-1}
\le
r_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)
\le
9.60339\times10^{-2},
\]
with the maximum at \(D=11\) and the minimum at \(D=7\). Hence the negative-fraction factor satisfies
\[
4.51983\times10^{-1}
\le
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\le
6.01894\times10^{-1},
\]
with the minimum again at \(D=11\). The absolute overlap-band floor is
\[
\min_D M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
6.45299\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=19\), while the negative overlap-band floor remains
\[
\min_D M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
3.01270\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=20\).

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O34 (The remaining overlap-side target is the absolute overlap-band mass).}
By Proposition~O35, one has the exact identity
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
=
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D),
\qquad
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)=\frac{1-r_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)}{2}.
\]
Since
\[
r_{\rm band}^{\rm(sgn)}(D)\le 9.60339\times10^{-2},
\]
it follows that
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\ge
(4.51983\times10^{-1})\,M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D).
\]
Combining this with the audited absolute overlap-band floor gives the direct lower bound
\[
M_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)
\ge
(4.51983\times10^{-1})(6.45299\times10^{-3})
=
2.91664\times10^{-3}.
\]
Compared with the exact negative overlap-band floor
\[
3.01270\times10^{-3},
\]
this misses by only the factor
\[
\frac{3.01270\times10^{-3}}{2.91664\times10^{-3}}
=
1.03294.
\]
Thus the support problem, the kernel problem, and the radial-weight problem are all closed. What remains on the overlap side is no longer the clipped negative carrier itself, but the absolute localized overlap-band mass
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
2\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} |\phi_R\phi_L|\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
with only a small signed-imbalance factor left outside it.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O36 (The absolute overlap-band mass factorizes through a same-center carrier moment and a weighted mirror-tail average).}
Keep the same canonical band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
and the localized basis \((\phi_R,\phi_L)\). On the canonical band, define the same-center and mirror amplitudes by
\[
\Phi_{\rm self}(\rho,z;D)
:=
|\phi_R(\rho,z;D)|\,\mathbf 1_{B_+(D)}
+
|\phi_L(\rho,z;D)|\,\mathbf 1_{B_-(D)},
\]
\[
\Phi_{\rm mir}(\rho,z;D)
:=
|\phi_L(\rho,z;D)|\,\mathbf 1_{B_+(D)}
+
|\phi_R(\rho,z;D)|\,\mathbf 1_{B_-(D)}.
\]
Then, by the localized-overlap identity from Proposition~O35,
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
2\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
\Phi_{\rm self}(\rho,z;D)\,\Phi_{\rm mir}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Define the same-center and mirror carrier moments
\[
S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
\Phi_{\rm self}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}
\Phi_{\rm mir}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
and the weighted local averages
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
:=
\frac{M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)}{2\,S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)},
\qquad
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self|mir)}(D)
:=
\frac{M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)}{2\,S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)}.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_band_absolute_overlap_source.py}
verifies the exact factorization
\[
\max_D\left|M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}-2\,S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}\right|
=
0,
\]
and equivalently
\[
\max_D\left|M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}-2\,S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self|mir)}\right|
=
0.
\]
On the audited window, the same-center carrier floor is
\[
\min_D S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
=
1.66599\times10^{-1}
\]
at \(D=20\), while the weighted mirror-tail average satisfies
\[
1.92259\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
\le
5.20575\times10^{-2},
\]
with the minimum at \(D=19\). Therefore the self-carrier route gives the induced product floor
\[
2\bigl(\min_D S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\bigr)
\bigl(\min_D \bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\bigr)
=
6.40606\times10^{-3}.
\]
The reverse route is weaker: although
\[
\min_D S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
5.79226\times10^{-2}
\]
at \(D=20\) and
\[
5.30602\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self|mir)}(D)
\le
7.34746\times10^{-2},
\]
the corresponding product floor is only
\[
6.14677\times10^{-3}.
\]
Hence the tighter canonical factorization is the same-center-carrier / weighted-mirror route.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O35 (The absolute overlap-band mass is already essentially fixed by the same-center carrier route).}
By Proposition~O36,
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
2\,S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D).
\]
Using the audited floors
\[
S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\ge 1.66599\times10^{-1},
\qquad
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\ge 1.92259\times10^{-2},
\]
one gets the direct lower bound
\[
M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
\ge
2(1.66599\times10^{-1})(1.92259\times10^{-2})
=
6.40606\times10^{-3}.
\]
The exact audited absolute overlap-band floor is
\[
\min_D M_{\rm band}^{\rm(abs)}(D)
=
6.45299\times10^{-3}
\]
at \(D=19\), so this misses the exact floor by only the factor
\[
\frac{6.45299\times10^{-3}}{6.40606\times10^{-3}}
=
1.00733.
\]
Therefore the remaining overlap-side proof target has narrowed again. It is no longer the full absolute overlap-band mass as an undifferentiated object, but the weighted mirror-tail average
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D),
\]
with the same-center carrier moment already providing an almost saturated one-center positive source.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O37 (The weighted mirror-tail average factorizes through an ordinary mirror-band mean and a self-weight bias).}
Keep the same canonical band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
\]
and define its band area
\[
A_{\rm band}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} 1\,d\rho\,dz.
\]
Using the mirror-tail carrier moment
\[
S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)} \Phi_{\rm mir}(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
define the ordinary mirror-band mean
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
:=
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)}{A_{\rm band}(D)}.
\]
Then the weighted mirror-tail average from Proposition~O36 satisfies the exact identity
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
=
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D),
\]
where
\[
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
:=
\frac{\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)}{\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)}
=
\frac{A_{\rm band}(D)\int \Phi_{\rm self}\Phi_{\rm mir}}
{\left(\int \Phi_{\rm self}\right)\left(\int \Phi_{\rm mir}\right)}.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_band_mirror_average_source.py}
verifies this factorization with zero residual:
\[
\max_D\left|\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}-\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}\right|
=
0.
\]
It finds
\[
2.43229\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
\le
6.23458\times10^{-2},
\]
with the floor at \(D=20\), while the self-weight bias lies in the interval
\[
7.40007\times10^{-1}
\le
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
\le
8.35352\times10^{-1},
\]
with the minimum at \(D=5\) and the maximum at \(D=9\). The band area itself is almost fixed:
\[
2.3814
\le
A_{\rm band}(D)
\le
2.3976,
\]
with relative spread only
\[
6.78914\times10^{-3}.
\]
Thus the weighted mirror-tail average differs from the ordinary mirror-band mean only by a positive self-weight bias and a tiny geometric area aliasing.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O36 (The remaining multiplier-side target can be taken to be the ordinary mirror-band mean).}
By Proposition~O37,
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
=
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D).
\]
Using the audited floors
\[
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\ge 7.40007\times10^{-1},
\qquad
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)\ge 2.43229\times10^{-2},
\]
one gets the direct lower bound
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
\ge
(7.40007\times10^{-1})(2.43229\times10^{-2})
=
1.79991\times10^{-2}.
\]
Compared with the exact weighted mirror-tail floor
\[
\min_D \bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
=
1.92259\times10^{-2},
\]
this misses by only the factor
\[
\frac{1.92259\times10^{-2}}{1.79991\times10^{-2}}
=
1.06816.
\]
Since \(A_{\rm band}(D)\) varies by less than one percent, the same statement may equivalently be read in terms of the mirror carrier moment \(S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}\), at the cost of only a tiny geometric aliasing. Therefore the remaining overlap-side multiplier target is no longer the self-weighted mirror average itself, but the ordinary mirror-band mean
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)}{A_{\rm band}(D)},
\]
with the self-weight bias confined to a narrow positive interval.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O38 (The ordinary mirror-band mean factors exactly through the same-center band mean and the mirror/self carrier ratio).}
Keep the same canonical band
\[
B_{\rm neg}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D).
\]
Define the same-center band mean
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
:=
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)}{A_{\rm band}(D)}
\]
and the mirror/self carrier ratio
\[
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
:=
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)}{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)}.
\]
Then the ordinary mirror-band mean satisfies the exact identity
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D).
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_diag_anchor_band_mirror_ratio_source.py}
verifies this factorization with machine-level residual
\[
\max_D\left|
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}
-
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}
\right|
=
6.94\times10^{-18}.
\]
It finds
\[
6.99586\times10^{-2}
\le
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)
\le
8.80198\times10^{-2},
\]
with the floor at \(D=20\), while the carrier ratio lies in the interval
\[
3.47676\times10^{-1}
\le
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)
\le
7.08509\times10^{-1},
\]
with the minimum at \(D=20\) and the maximum at \(D=4\). The ordinary mirror-band mean itself has audited floor
\[
\min_D \bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
2.43229\times10^{-2}
\]
at the same knot \(D=20\).

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O37 (The ordinary mirror-band mean is already saturated by the same-center band mean and the mirror/self ratio).}
By Proposition~O38,
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\,\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D).
\]
Using the audited floors
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\ge 6.99586\times10^{-2},
\qquad
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\ge 3.47676\times10^{-1},
\]
one gets
\[
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
\ge
(6.99586\times10^{-2})(3.47676\times10^{-1})
=
2.43229\times10^{-2}.
\]
This equals the exact audited floor
\[
\min_D \bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}(D)
=
2.43229\times10^{-2},
\]
up to machine precision, with all three floor knots aligned at \(D=20\). Therefore the ordinary mirror-band mean is no longer a substantive multiplier-side blocker on the current exact export: it is already closed by a same-center positive carrier and a mirror/self ratio, with no further support refinement required.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O39 (Synthesis of the overlap-side reduction chain on the canonical exact bridge).}
This proposition is the reviewer-facing form of the whole overlap-side ladder.  The preceding Propositions~O19--O38 should be read as an audit trail which proves the displayed factorization and its leakage terms; they do not introduce additional theorem objects beyond the canonical band, its carrier moments, and the already-fixed exact bridge.
Combining Propositions~O19--O38, the canonical adverse-band piece of the interior one-center mass-sector cancellation admits the exact composite factorization
\[
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)
=
4\pi\,
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,
\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}(D)\,
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)\,
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}(D)\,
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}(D)^2}{A_{\rm band}(D)}.
\]
Equivalently, the normalized interior mass-sector cancellation ratio satisfies
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(D)
=
\frac{N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
\frac{N_{\rm mass,core}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
\frac{N_{\rm mass,rim}^{(0.20,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}
+
\frac{N_{\rm mass,tail}^{(0.15,1.20)}(D)}{P_{\rm mass,in}(D)}.
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_parity_contrast_synthesis_source.py}
verifies both identities on the exact audited knot set with residuals
\[
\max_D\left|
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}
-
4\pi\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)}\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)}\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)}
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)}\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)}
\frac{S_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)\,2}}{A_{\rm band}}
\right|
=
9.67\times10^{-17},
\]
\[
\max_D\left|
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}
-
\frac{N_{\rm mass,band}}{P_{\rm mass,in}}
-
\frac{N_{\rm mass,core}}{P_{\rm mass,in}}
-
\frac{N_{\rm mass,rim}}{P_{\rm mass,in}}
-
\frac{N_{\rm mass,tail}}{P_{\rm mass,in}}
\right|
=
5.97\times10^{-14}.
\]
At the canonical adverse-band floor knot \(D=19\), the composite mainline factor already reproduces the exact band magnitude:
\[
N_{\rm mass,band}^{(0.20;0.10,1.20)}(19)
=
7.37447\times10^{-4},
\]
with residual \(2.72\times10^{-17}\). At the worst interior cancellation knot \(D=11\), the normalized decomposition reads
\[
\nu_{\rm diag,int}^{(\pm,m)}(11)
=
0.813941
+
4.19214\times10^{-2}
+
5.38319\times10^{-2}
+
4.81178\times10^{-2}
=
0.957812,
\]
so the audited exact bridge is now reduced to one multiplicative mainline and three explicit leakage channels.
Condensed map: \(O19\text{--}O22\) close the one-center/core/anchor localization, \(O23\text{--}O32\) close the support and kernel reductions, \(O33\text{--}O38\) close the carrier and overlap-factor identities up to constants, and \(O39\) is the single synthesis statement.  Thus a reader may verify the proof at the level of this proposition plus the audit residuals above, and use the earlier propositions only for reproducibility of the individual factors.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O38 (What is closed, and what remains only as secondary tightening).}
On the current exact export, the overlap-side chain from Propositions~O19--O38 now separates cleanly into \emph{closed} structural blocks and \emph{secondary tightening} blocks.

\emph{Closed on the audited exact bridge:}
\[
\text{support reduction }(O19\text{--}O32),\qquad
P_{\rm mass,in},\qquad
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)},\qquad
\Lambda_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir|self)},\qquad
\bar\Phi_{\rm band}^{\rm(mir)}.
\]
In particular, Proposition~O38 shows that the ordinary mirror-band mean is already saturated exactly by the same-center band mean and the mirror/self carrier ratio at the common knot \(D=20\), so no further support/object refinement is needed on this line.

\emph{Only secondary tightening remains in:}
\[
\bar K_{\rm band}^{(-)},\qquad
\bar\rho_{\rm band}^{(-)},\qquad
\theta_{\rm band}^{(-)},\qquad
\Xi_{\rm band}^{\rm(self)},\qquad
\frac{N_{\rm mass,core}}{P_{\rm mass,in}},\qquad
\frac{N_{\rm mass,rim}}{P_{\rm mass,in}},\qquad
\frac{N_{\rm mass,tail}}{P_{\rm mass,in}},
\]
with the outer same-sign parity-contrast remainder \(I_\times\) from Proposition~O19 also retained only as a small static correction. Therefore the mainline task is no longer to discover a new support or a new overlap object. The chain is already structurally closed; what remains, if any further improvement is desired, is only constant-tightening inside the already identified multiplicative and leakage factors.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Benchmark O1 (Dense-family sharpness check).}
The dense exact-family scan should now be read as a sharpness benchmark rather than as the theorem input. Resolving the same family on
\[
\alpha,\beta\in[0.95,1.05],
\qquad
\lambda\in[-0.2,0.2],
\]
with step `0.005` and imposing exact admissibility $J_{\rm parent}\le 10^{-6}$ shows that the extremal ridge sits near
\[
(\alpha,\beta,\lambda)\approx(1.03,0.99,\pm0.2),
\]
giving
\[
u_a\le 5.42\times10^{-3},
\qquad
\mathrm{tr}\,A_a\le 5.42\times10^{-3},
\qquad
\|A_a\|\le 5.42\times10^{-3},
\]
with
\[
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9946\,H_{PP,a},
\qquad
\left|\log\det H_{{\rm eff},a}-\log\det H_{PP,a}\right|
\le 1.09\times10^{-2}.
\]
This is the tightest local benchmark currently available, but it is only about one percent above Proposition~O5. Even the conservative raw off-diagonal envelope remains moderate on the same dense admissible window, giving only
\[
u_a\le 7.19\times10^{-3},
\qquad
\left|\log\det H_{{\rm eff},a}-\log\det H_{PP,a}\right|
\le 1.45\times10^{-2}.
\]
So the right reading is now stable: the dense exact-family scan sets the benchmark, while the continuum neighborhood bound is the theorem candidate to push beyond the scanned family.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O40 (Synthesis of the dominant-pole source chain on the audited exact bridge).}
Combining Propositions~O1--O9, Corollaries~O1--O8, and Benchmark~O1, the dominant-pole line on the current audited exact bridge separates into three structural blocks:

with the envelope attained by the exact factorization-side block-22 witness at \(D=13\), and

by factors

Meanwhile the family-transfer audit still gives

N_{\rm adm}^{\rm(noncan)}=0

at \(J_\ast=10^{-6}\), with the minimum selection gap exceeding that theorem tolerance by the factor

Thus, on the current exact bridge, the defect side, the family/chart transfer side, and the exact witness-envelope side are already mutually aligned: the only missing step is no longer to discover a new response family or a new projected interaction channel, but to promote the same determinant-deficit budget from audited evidence to an a priori exact Schur-resolvent lift.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O39 (What is closed, and what remains as the single dominant-pole gap).}
On the current audited exact bridge, the dominant-pole / Schur-resolvent line now separates cleanly into \emph{closed} structural blocks and \emph{one} remaining theorem-level lift.

\emph{Closed on the audited exact bridge:}
\[
P\Delta V P\in{\rm span}\{I,\sigma_z,\sigma_x\},
\qquad
\delta_{q,a}\approx 0,
\qquad
\text{family/chart transfer},

Equivalently, the localized two-level reduction, the gap-locked single-pole response family, the on-shell defect closure, and the exact operator witness inclusion are no longer substantive blockers on the current knot set.

\emph{The single remaining theorem-level gap is:}

u_a^{(\mathrm{neigh})}\le 5.34\times10^{-3}
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339

from the present audited projected-family / chart evidence to a genuine parent-side analytic lift for the exact Schur resolvent.  Once that step is made, Theorem Target~O1 is closed on the current on-shell window without introducing any new support, response family, or overlap object.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O41 (A single scalar sufficient condition for the exact Schur lift).}
The remaining theorem-level lift of Corollary~O39 can be reduced one step further to a single scalar lower floor on the mix kernel.  Define
\[
m_{{\rm mix},\ast}
:=
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}},
\qquad
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}
:=
\max_D\bigl|\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)-\kappa_{\rm env}\,g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\bigr|.
\]
On the current audited exact bridge,
\[
\varepsilon_{\rm mix}=4.40\times10^{-14},
\qquad
\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}=4.81\times10^{-5},
\]
so
\[
m_{{\rm mix},\ast}=9.15\times10^{-10}.
\]
Suppose a parent-side analytic argument supplies the uniform lower floor
\[
g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge m_{{\rm mix},\ast}
\]
throughout the audited on-shell window.  Then Proposition~O9 gives
\[
\|\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(fact)}\|_\infty
\le
\frac{\varepsilon_{\rm mix}}{m_{{\rm mix},\ast}}
\le
\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}.
\]
Meanwhile all non-mix witness components are already much smaller:
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(nonmix)}
:=
\max\bigl\{
\Delta_{\phi}^{\rm(fact)},
\Delta_{\rm tr}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm det}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm ani}^{\rm(stmt)},
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(cocycle)},
\Delta_{\rm mix}^{\rm(potential)}
\bigr\}
=2.33\times10^{-10},
\]
so
\[
\frac{\tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe}}{\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(nonmix)}}
=2.06\times10^{5}.
\]
Hence the exact witness envelope satisfies
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm op}^{\rm(exact)}\le \tfrac12 r_{95}^{\rm safe},
\]
the exact operator lies in the audited safe chart by Corollary~O7, and the determinant-deficit budget of Proposition~O5 transfers to the exact Schur-reduced operator. Therefore
\[
u_a\le 5.34\times10^{-3},
\qquad
g_a^{\rm(exact)}\ge 1.3339,
\qquad
H_{{\rm eff},a}\succeq 0.9947\,H_{PP,a},
\]
and, together with Corollary~O2, Theorem Target~O1 is closed on the current on-shell window.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O40 (The exact Schur lift has been reduced to one scalar floor).}
On the current audited exact bridge, the only remaining analytic input needed for the dominant-pole / exact-Schur lift is any one parent-side proof of a mix-kernel floor at or above
\[
g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge 9.15\times10^{-10}.
\]
Equivalently, since \(\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}=4.31\times10^{5}\) on the same knot set, it is enough to prove
\[
\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)\ge 3.94\times10^{-4}.
\]
The new audit \path{code/audit_open_system_exact_schur_lift_scalar_source.py}
shows that the current exported exact bridge already exceeds this threshold in several independent ways:
\[
\min_D g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)=1.2147\times10^{-9},
\qquad
\frac{\min_D g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)}{m_{{\rm mix},\ast}}=1.328,
\]
\[
\frac{\min_D\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)}{\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}
=1.2147\times10^{-9},
\qquad
\frac{\min_D\gamma_{\rm mix}(D)/\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}{m_{{\rm mix},\ast}}=1.328,
\]
and even the separated observable-chain floor already gives
\[
\frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}}{\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}
=1.0016\times10^{-9},
\qquad
\frac{\gamma_{\rm mix}^{\rm(sep\ floor)}/\kappa_{\rm env}^{\max}}{m_{{\rm mix},\ast}}=1.095.
\]
Thus the exact Schur-lift problem is no longer a multi-parameter chart or support problem: it has been reduced to one scalar analytic floor on the mix kernel (equivalently on \(\gamma_{\rm mix}\)), while the defect side, the family/chart transfer side, and the non-mix witness side are already closed on the audited exact bridge.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O42 (The remaining scalar Schur-lift gap is only a small tightening of the current static parity floor).}
The scalar threshold of Proposition~O41 can be compared directly with the current overlap-side lower bounds.  By Corollary~O20,
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|\ge 5.64043\times10^{-5}
\]
on the audited exact bridge, hence by Proposition~O18

\]
Combining this with the global response floor from Proposition~O17,

gives the exact-bridge lower estimate

\kappa_{\rm env}\,(2.820215\times10^{-5})^2\,S_{xx}^{\rm floor}

\path{code/audit_open_system_exact_schur_lift_overlap_bridge.py}

would be sufficient to close Proposition~O41.

If one instead keeps the exact local response factor at the common knot \(D=11\),

then the same parity floor already gives

\qquad
\frac{3.69779\times10^{-4}}{3.93829\times10^{-4}}=0.93893,
\]
so the remaining gap is only
\[
g_x(D)\ge 1.03201\times(2.820215\times10^{-5})
\]
under that local-response alignment.  Likewise, using the slightly stronger core-window parity floor
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|\ge 5.70400\times10^{-5}
\]
from Corollary~O19 improves the global-response fraction to
\[
0.90250
\]
and the local-response fraction to
\[
0.96022.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O41 (The exact Schur-lift gap is no longer a new operator question, but a small constant-tightening question on the static overlap side).}
On the current exact bridge, Propositions~O41--O42 show that the remaining dominant-pole / exact-Schur lift gap is not a missing chart inclusion, not a missing response family, and not a missing non-mix operator estimate.  It is already reduced to a small scalar constant gap between the current static parity-contrast floor and the threshold

g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge 9.15\times10^{-10}.

With the globally valid response floor from Proposition~O17, the remaining deficit is only a

tightening of the compact-anchor \(g_x\) floor, and even on the exact local-response alignment it drops to only

\]
So the next useful proof-side task is now very specific: strengthen the current one-center/static parity floor just enough to cross the scalar threshold of Proposition~O41, rather than reopening any new Schur-resolvent structure.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O43 (The scalar Schur-lift criterion is equivalent to a single static parity threshold).}
Proposition~O41 asks only for the scalar floor

g_x(D)^2S_{xx}(D)\ge m_{{\rm mix},\ast},

m_{{\rm mix},\ast}=9.14719\times10^{-10}.

\ge
2\sqrt{\frac{m_{{\rm mix},\ast}}{S_\ast}}.

the exact Schur lift follows from the single static bound

|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|\ge 6.00420\times10^{-5}.
\]
With the exact local-response alignment at the common knot \(D=11\),

S_\ast=S_{xx}(D_{g_x,\min})=1.07984,
\]
the required static threshold sharpens to
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|\ge 5.82096\times10^{-5}.

These two thresholds can then be compared directly with the currently available overlap-side floors:

|V_{11}-V_{22}|_{\rm anchor}=5.64043\times10^{-5},
\qquad

\]
\[

\]
Numerically, the current compact-anchor theorem already reaches
\[
93.94\%
\quad\text{of the global threshold,}
\qquad
96.90\%
\quad\text{of the local-response threshold,}
\]
while the current core-window theorem reaches
\[
95.00\%
\quad\text{of the global threshold,}
\qquad
97.99\%
\quad\text{of the local-response threshold.}
\]
Meanwhile the exact bridge floor already exceeds the same thresholds by the factors
\[

\quad\text{(global-response version)},
\qquad

\quad\text{(local-response version)}.

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O42 (The remaining dominant-pole theorem gap is exactly a one-scalar parity-threshold tightening).}
Propositions~O41--O43 show that the residual exact Schur-lift task can now be stated with no additional response-family or chart language.  It is enough to strengthen the current static parity theorem to one of the two scalar thresholds

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O44 (The same core-window family already gives a tighter floor for the exact total parity contrast).}
Keep the same symmetric core-window family \(B_{\rm core}(R;D)\) from Proposition~O20, but apply it directly to the full exact parity-contrast density:
\[
J_{\rm core}^{(R)}(D)
:=
\int_{B_{\rm core}(R;D)}
\mathcal C(\rho,z;D)\,\delta V(\rho,z;D)\,d\rho\,dz,
\]
\[
J_{\rm tail}^{(R)}(D)
:=
\bigl(V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\bigr)-J_{\rm core}^{(R)}(D).
\]
Thus
\[
V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)=J_{\rm core}^{(R)}(D)+J_{\rm tail}^{(R)}(D).
\]
The audit
\path{code/audit_open_system_exact_total_core_tightening_source.py}
rescans the same interval
\[
R\in[1.20,1.40]
\]
with the exact total density instead of the one-center pair alone.  It finds that the same optimal safe radius remains
\[
R_\ast=1.35,
\]
and now satisfies on every audited knot \(D=4,\dots,20\)
\[
\operatorname{sgn}J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)
=
\operatorname{sgn}\bigl(V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\bigr),
\]
\[
\operatorname{sgn}J_{\rm tail}^{(R_\ast)}(D)
=
-\operatorname{sgn}\bigl(V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)\bigr).
\]
Hence
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|
=
|J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
-|J_{\rm tail}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
\ge
\lambda_{\rm tot,\ast}\,|J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|,
\]
with
\[
\lambda_{\rm tot,\ast}=0.426709\ldots
\]
and
\[
\min_D |J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(D)|
=
1.34704\times10^{-4}.
\]
Therefore the same core-window family already yields the sharper exact-total floor
\[
|V_{11}(D)-V_{22}(D)|
\ge
5.74795\times10^{-5}.
\]

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O46 (The parent-side analytic lift has been reduced to the same family and two windowwise core/tail bounds).}

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O47 (The remaining parent-side task is only a left-window bottleneck bound, equivalent to either a tail-ratio theorem or a core-floor theorem).}

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O50 (After the audited hierarchical split, the remaining parent-side lift is equivalent to a single-knot core-floor theorem at \(D=11\)).}

which exceeds these single-knot targets by the factors

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O51 (At the singleton bottleneck knot, the exact core is already carried by the same-center pair core, with only a tiny same-sign cross refinement).}

where the first two terms come from the two one-center same-sign pieces and the last term is the cross correction, all integrated on the same core region \(B_{\rm core}(R_\ast;11)\).  The audit

Meanwhile the core cross correction is

J_{\times,{\rm core}}^{(R_\ast)}(11)
=
8.91952\times10^{-7},

has the same sign as \(J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(11)\), and contributes only

and the audited same-center pair value exceeds them by the factors

\qquad\text{and}\qquad

\medskip\noindent\textbf{Corollary O50 (The remaining parent-side theorem target can be narrowed once more to a same-center pair-core lower bound at \(D=11\)).}
On the audited bridge, the singleton bottleneck no longer requires a theorem for the full exact core \(J_{\rm core}^{(R_\ast)}(11)\). \medskip\noindent\textbf{Proposition O52 (At \(D=11\), the same-center pair core is already carried by the canonical compact anchor, and that anchor is itself carried by a same-center static anchor up to a local mirror loss).}
Keep the same singleton knot \(D=11\), the same radius \(R_\ast=1.35\), and the same canonical compact anchor
